-- Sent the very first thing the script does, then a pause before
-- anything else happens.
local START_COMMAND = "This reanimate was made by the DinoKid himself"
local START_WAIT    = 2
-- Also send it at the start of every respawn's boot.
local START_COMMAND_ON_RESPAWN = true

-- Seconds to wait after every rig part is present before sending the
-- commands below (lets the accessories finish settling on you), and
-- after the commands before the body is split.
local CONFIRM_WAIT  = 2
local POST_CMD_WAIT = 2

-- Game commands sent once per life, AFTER all rig parts have arrived:
--   -net  the game keeps your hats' network ownership on you instead of
--         handing them to other players (the real fix for lost parts)
--   -pd   permadeath, so the server doesn't respawn you mid-reanimate
-- Sent once per character only: if a command is a toggle, sending it
-- again would switch it back off.
local BOOT_COMMANDS = { "-net", "-pd" }

-- Hats to bring back if the game deletes them. Put the catalog ids of
-- the hats you wear here, e.g. { "1234567890" }. When one of your hats
-- disappears, "-gh <these ids>" is sent and the new copy is put straight
-- back on you (it rides the limb its attachment says, like any new
-- accessory). Leave empty to only log.
local KEEP_HAT_IDS = {}

local BOOT_COMMAND_GAP = 1   -- seconds between chat commands (chat throttles)

local GH_WAIT = 10
-- Extra seconds to keep polling if some parts are still missing.
local RIG_EXTRA_WAIT = 8
-- respawnrequest() reassigns lp.Character, which the server takes a
-- moment to process. Splitting the body before that settles makes the
-- Health = 0 read as a genuine death and you get respawned. The old
-- -gh wait was masking this; keep an explicit one.

--==================================================
-- CHAT
--==================================================

local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
local TChannels       = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
local RBXGeneral      = TChannels and TChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")

local function send(msg)
    -- One chat line every 1.5 s at most, shared by every copy of the
    -- script, so Roblox's flood guard never swallows a command. The
    -- slot is reserved before waiting, so lines queue in order.
    local now  = os.clock()
    local slot = math.max(now, tonumber(_G.DinoNextChatAt) or 0)
    _G.DinoNextChatAt = slot + 1.5
    if slot > now then task.wait(slot - now) end

    local ok = pcall(function() lp:Chat(msg) end)
    if not ok and RBXGeneral then
        pcall(function() RBXGeneral:SendAsync(msg) end)
    end
end


--==================================================
-- CONFIG
--==================================================

local CONFIG = {
    animSpeed  = 1.0,
    swingSpeed = 1.0,
    blendTime  = 0.18,   -- crossfade duration between animations

    -- Drive mode.
    --
    -- true  : velocity matches the part's real motion. Smooth for
    --         everyone else, because their interpolation follows
    --         the same path yours does.
    -- false : original constant-lift netless. Included so you can
    --         switch back if a game rejects the smooth version.
    smoothDrive = true,

    -- Clamps, so one oversized frame delta cannot read as a launch
    -- on another client.
    maxDriveSpeed = 120,
    maxDriveSpin  = 45,

    -- Replication smoothing (what other players see).
    --
    -- antiGravity    Cancels gravity on every driven part in YOUR
    --                simulation. Without it the solver bleeds ~3.3
    --                studs/s of downward velocity into every part each
    --                frame, and that sagging velocity is what gets sent
    --                to other players, bowing each part downward between
    --                network updates.
    -- velSmoothing   0..0.9. Blends each frame's velocity with the last
    --                one. Leave at 0: velocity is now derived from the
    --                root's physics velocity, which has no frame-time
    --                noise to filter, and any blending makes the sent
    --                velocity lag behind the body on starts and stops,
    --                which is exactly the forward/back jitter.
    -- ownershipDebug Logs every time the server takes one of your parts
    --                away and gives it back (needs isnetworkowner, which
    --                Potassium has). Shows live in the status strip too.
    antiGravity    = true,
    velSmoothing   = 0,
    ownershipDebug = true,

    -- Part survival in busy servers (taken from how Uhhhhhh's reanimate
    -- keeps hats alive).
    --
    -- netlessLift  Every driven part reports an upward velocity of ~25
    --              studs/s (horizontal stays the real motion). Nothing
    --              moves up: the position is pinned back to the pose after
    --              every physics step. It only matters the moment another
    --              player or the server takes a part over: the last
    --              velocity they got from you is UP, so the part floats
    --              instead of dropping into the void, and you reclaim it
    --              seconds later. With real velocity only (the old smooth
    --              mode) a standing still part reported 0 and fell the
    --              instant ownership flipped, which is what kept eating
    --              parts with people nearby. Costs a little smoothness for
    --              other players; turn off in the RIG tab to compare.
    -- voidGuard    Sets YOUR workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight to NaN
    --              every frame, so your own simulation never deletes a
    --              part it owns that dipped low, and clamps every pose
    --              above the real void line.
    netlessLift = true,
    voidGuard   = true,

    -- Camera (also adjustable live in the CAMERA tab).
    --
    -- cameraFollow   "head" : camera focus rides your animated head, so
    --                         it bobs and dips with the pose
    --                "root" : stock behaviour, fixed to the driver root
    -- cameraAxes     "vertical" : up/down only
    --                "full"     : up/down + lean + forward/back sway
    -- cameraStrength 0 = none, 1 = exact head motion, >1 exaggerates
    -- cameraSmooth   0 = locked to the head (recommended). Anything
    --                above 0 makes the camera trail the head, which
    --                reads as the body lagging on screen.
    cameraFollow   = "head",
    cameraAxes     = "vertical",
    cameraStrength = 1.0,
    cameraSmooth   = 0,

    -- Left click plays the swing animation. Off by default.
    clickToSwing   = false,
}


--==================================================
-- RIG
--==================================================
--
-- Each entry maps one R6 limb to the instance that replaces it.
-- name matches an Accessory, a BasePart or a Model anywhere in your
-- character. rx/ry/rz (degrees) and ox/oy/oz (studs) are applied in the
-- limb's own space on top of its pose:
--     rigCF = limbPose * CFrame.new(ox, oy, oz) * CFrame.Angles(rx, ry, rz)
-- All zero by default: the rig meshes already sit upright on their limbs.
--==================================================

local RIG = {
    -- false = your REAL body parts are the avatar (no -gh, no rig
    --         accessories; your real limbs are posed and stay visible).
    -- true  = the autorig accessories replace your limbs (old behaviour).
    enabled = false,

    -- "void" is the default, like Uhhhhhh's hat reanimate: the real
    -- limbs are parked straight under you, just above the void line, far
    -- below the map where no one can see them. If someone else ever takes
    -- one over it simply drops out of the world, which costs nothing:
    -- those limbs are hidden and nothing depends on them any more.
    -- "tuck" packs them inside the rig torso (visible if the rig torso is
    -- smaller than an R6 torso). "under" parks them 12 studs down.
    realBodyMode = "void",    -- "void" | "tuck" | "under" | "overlay"
    voidHeight   = 15,        -- studs above the void line for "void"
    underDepth   = 12,        -- studs below the root for "under"

    -- Self-heal when a rig part is lost in a busy server:
    --   1. a spare copy of that part (if you have one) takes over instantly
    --   2. otherwise -gh is re-sent and the replacement binds on arrival
    -- Duplicates that arrive for limbs already bound are kept as spares,
    -- parked with the hidden real body so they're never seen.
    autoRegh      = true,
    reghCooldown  = 8,        -- seconds between automatic -gh sends

    parts = {
        { limb = "Torso",     name = "Accessory (autorig_Torso)",    rx = 0,   ry = 0, rz = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0, oz = 0 },
        { limb = "Head",      name = "Dummy_Head",                   rx = 0,   ry = 0, rz = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0, oz = 0 },
        { limb = "Left Arm",  name = "Accessory (autorig_LeftArm)",  rx = 0,   ry = 0, rz = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0, oz = 0 },
        { limb = "Right Arm", name = "Accessory (autorig_RightArm)", rx = 0,   ry = 0, rz = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0, oz = 0 },
        { limb = "Left Leg",  name = "Accessory (autorig_LeftLeg)",  rx = 0,   ry = 0, rz = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0, oz = 0 },
        { limb = "Right Leg", name = "Accessory (autorig_RightLeg)", rx = 0,   ry = 0, rz = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0, oz = 0 },
    },
}

local REAL_BODY_MODES = { "void", "tuck", "under", "overlay" }

local function rigEntryByName(n)
    for _, rp in ipairs(RIG.parts) do
        if rp.name == n then return rp end
    end
    return nil
end

-- Filled by the embedded anti-fling (bottom of the file). setup() calls
-- AntiFling.Rebind() after the split so it protects the driver you
-- walk with instead of the dead corpse.
local AntiFling: { [string]: any } = {}

-- DEANIMATE / HIDE BODY PARTS / ROBLOX ANIMATIONS share one table,
-- because the main chunk is close to Luau's 200-local limit.
local Ext: { [string]: any } = {
    enabled   = true,       -- false while deanimated (a normal player)
    busy      = nil,        -- "deanimating" while the respawn is on its way
    oldChar   = nil,        -- the reanimated body being left behind
    returnCF  = nil,        -- where the normal body is put back
    hidden    = {},         -- [limb or part name] = true: parked far below you
    animMode  = "custom",   -- "custom" = your keyframes · "roblox" = Roblox defaults
    rbx       = { tracks = {} },
    listeners = {},

    -- This game respawns you on a chat command, and its -pd permadeath
    -- only ends through one: -rs refreshes you where you stand, -re
    -- respawns you. Tried in order until a new body arrives; the
    -- engine's character reassign is the last resort.
    respawnCommands = { "-rs", "-re" },
    respawnWait     = 6,     -- seconds to wait for a body after each try

    -- bodies the respawn handler has already taken care of
    handled = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }),

    -- 3D animator (ANIMATE tab → 3D EDIT)
    giz         = { mode = "rotate", snap = true, cam = false, speed = 16, undo = {} },
    gizmoFrames = {},     -- [track] = { pivot = CFrame, offset = CFrame }, from DriveFrame
    gizmoOn     = false,
}

-- RE-RUN GUARD. Running the script again replaces this copy: every
-- loop, hook and boot of the old copy checks Ext.alive() and stops, so
-- nothing is driven, booted or sent to chat twice.
Ext.session = {}
_G.AutorigReanimSession = Ext.session
function Ext.alive()
    return _G.AutorigReanimSession == Ext.session
end

-- Chat lines already sent per body, shared by every copy: a re-run
-- never repeats the start line, -gh, -net or -pd for the same body
-- (a second -pd would switch permadeath back off).
_G.DinoSentFor = _G.DinoSentFor or setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
function Ext.sendOnce(body, msg)
    if body then
        local sent = _G.DinoSentFor[body]
        if not sent then
            sent = {}
            _G.DinoSentFor[body] = sent
        end
        if sent[msg] then return false end
        sent[msg] = true
    end
    send(msg)
    return true
end

-- ANTIKILL's panel shows a DEANIMATE / REANIMATE button through this
AntiFling.Host = {
    State  = function() return Ext.state and Ext.state() or "starting" end,
    Toggle = function() if Ext.toggle then return Ext.toggle() end return nil end,
}

local function rigEntryByLimb(limb)
    for _, rp in ipairs(RIG.parts) do
        if rp.limb == limb then return rp end
    end
    return nil
end




--==================================================
-- LIMBS + POSE HELPERS

-- Real corpse body parts (what setup() hunts for in the model)
local LIMBS = { "Torso", "Head", "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg" }

--==================================================
-- R15
--==================================================
--
-- The body type is read from your Humanoid right before the split.
-- R15 bodies are driven joint by joint with forward kinematics, using
-- YOUR character's own Motor6D offsets (captured before the joints
-- break), so any body scale or proportion fits:
--     part = parent * C0 * Transform * C1:Inverse()
-- which is exactly how Roblox poses a Motor6D.
--
-- Where each joint's Transform comes from:
--   · animations imported from R15 keep their raw joint data
--     ("R15:<part>" tracks), so on an R15 body they play exactly like
--     the original: elbows, knees, wrists, waist, everything
--   · everything else (idle/walk/swing, R6 animations, your own edits)
--     uses the six R6 tracks: Torso drives the root joint, Head the
--     neck, each arm/leg its shoulder/hip, the rest stays straight
-- A blend weight track ("R15:_w") crossfades between the two, so
-- switching between an R15 import and an R6 animation never pops.
--==================================================

local IS_R15 = false

local R15_PARTS = {
    "LowerTorso", "UpperTorso", "Head",
    "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand",
    "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand",
    "LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot",
    "RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot",
}

-- Which R6 limb each R15 part belongs to (hiding, parking, status)
local R15_GROUP = {
    LowerTorso = "Torso", UpperTorso = "Torso", Head = "Head",
    LeftUpperArm = "Left Arm", LeftLowerArm = "Left Arm", LeftHand = "Left Arm",
    RightUpperArm = "Right Arm", RightLowerArm = "Right Arm", RightHand = "Right Arm",
    LeftUpperLeg = "Left Leg", LeftLowerLeg = "Left Leg", LeftFoot = "Left Leg",
    RightUpperLeg = "Right Leg", RightLowerLeg = "Right Leg", RightFoot = "Right Leg",
}

-- Which joint takes the R6 track (the rest stay straight)
local R15_DRIVER = {
    Head = "Head",
    LeftUpperArm = "Left Arm", RightUpperArm = "Right Arm",
    LeftUpperLeg = "Left Leg", RightUpperLeg = "Right Leg",
}

-- Standard R15 rig attachments: [child] = { parent, attachment name }.
-- Every R15 body part carries these no matter which joint system the
-- game uses, so they are the fallback when no Motor6D is found.
local R15_RIG = {
    LowerTorso    = { "HumanoidRootPart", "RootRigAttachment" },
    UpperTorso    = { "LowerTorso",    "WaistRigAttachment" },
    Head          = { "UpperTorso",    "NeckRigAttachment" },
    LeftUpperArm  = { "UpperTorso",    "LeftShoulderRigAttachment" },
    LeftLowerArm  = { "LeftUpperArm",  "LeftElbowRigAttachment" },
    LeftHand      = { "LeftLowerArm",  "LeftWristRigAttachment" },
    RightUpperArm = { "UpperTorso",    "RightShoulderRigAttachment" },
    RightLowerArm = { "RightUpperArm", "RightElbowRigAttachment" },
    RightHand     = { "RightLowerArm", "RightWristRigAttachment" },
    LeftUpperLeg  = { "LowerTorso",    "LeftHipRigAttachment" },
    LeftLowerLeg  = { "LeftUpperLeg",  "LeftKneeRigAttachment" },
    LeftFoot      = { "LeftLowerLeg",  "LeftAnkleRigAttachment" },
    RightUpperLeg = { "LowerTorso",    "RightHipRigAttachment" },
    RightLowerLeg = { "RightUpperLeg", "RightKneeRigAttachment" },
    RightFoot     = { "RightLowerLeg", "RightAnkleRigAttachment" },
}

-- Anything that can hold two body parts together. Newer Roblox R15
-- avatars (the avatar joint upgrade) use AnimationConstraint +
-- BallSocketConstraint instead of Motor6D, and BreakJoints() does NOT
-- remove those, so the body stayed one rigid piece.
local BODY_JOINT_CLASSES = {
    AnimationConstraint = true, BallSocketConstraint = true, HingeConstraint = true,
    RigidConstraint = true, RodConstraint = true, RopeConstraint = true,
    SpringConstraint = true, PrismaticConstraint = true, CylindricalConstraint = true,
    UniversalConstraint = true,
}

local function isBodyJoint(v)
    return v:IsA("JointInstance") or v:IsA("WeldConstraint")
        or BODY_JOINT_CLASSES[v.ClassName] == true
end

-- Extra joints the editor can pose on an R15 body (the six R6 tracks
-- already cover root/neck/shoulders/hips). Stored as "R15:<part>"
-- tracks: rotation in degrees + offset in studs, same as any limb.
local R15_EDIT = {
    "R15:UpperTorso",
    "R15:LeftLowerArm",  "R15:LeftHand",
    "R15:RightLowerArm", "R15:RightHand",
    "R15:LeftLowerLeg",  "R15:LeftFoot",
    "R15:RightLowerLeg", "R15:RightFoot",
}

-- Captured per character: [part] = { parent = name, c0 = CFrame, c1 = CFrame }
local R15Joints = {}
local R15Order  = {}

local function rawTrack(T)
    local x, y, z = T:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
    local p = T.Position
    return { rx = math.deg(x), ry = math.deg(y), rz = math.deg(z),
             ox = p.X, oy = p.Y, oz = p.Z }
end

-- Adds raw R15 joint tracks (+ the blend weight) to a pose
local function addRawR15(pose, transforms)
    local any = false
    for name, T in pairs(transforms) do
        if R15_GROUP[name] then
            pose["R15:" .. name] = rawTrack(T)
            any = true
        end
    end
    if any then
        pose["R15:_w"] = { rx = 1, ry = 0, rz = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0, oz = 0 }
    end
    return pose
end

-- Registered props: any accessory you add gets its own animation track.
-- Each rides a limb, has a resting grip, and the keyframe values layer
-- on top of that grip.
--   Props[i] = { id, accName, handle, attach,
--                gx,gy,gz, grx,gry,grz }
local Props = {}

-- Everything a keyframe can pose: the six limbs plus every prop id.
-- Rebuilt whenever a prop is added or removed.
local POSE_PARTS = { "Torso", "Head", "Left Arm", "Right Arm",
                     "Left Leg", "Right Leg" }

local function rebuildPoseParts()
    POSE_PARTS = { "Torso", "Head", "Left Arm", "Right Arm",
                   "Left Leg", "Right Leg" }
    if IS_R15 then
        for _, k in ipairs(R15_EDIT) do table.insert(POSE_PARTS, k) end
    end
    for _, pr in ipairs(Props) do
        table.insert(POSE_PARTS, pr.id)
    end
end

local function findProp(id)
    for i, pr in ipairs(Props) do
        if pr.id == id then return pr, i end
    end
    return nil
end

local LIMB_SHORT = {
    ["Torso"]     = "Torso",
    ["Head"]      = "Head",
    ["Left Arm"]  = "L Arm",
    ["Right Arm"] = "R Arm",
    ["Left Leg"]  = "L Leg",
    ["Right Leg"] = "R Leg",
    ["R15:UpperTorso"]    = "Waist",
    ["R15:LeftLowerArm"]  = "L Elbow",
    ["R15:LeftHand"]      = "L Wrist",
    ["R15:RightLowerArm"] = "R Elbow",
    ["R15:RightHand"]     = "R Wrist",
    ["R15:LeftLowerLeg"]  = "L Knee",
    ["R15:LeftFoot"]      = "L Ankle",
    ["R15:RightLowerLeg"] = "R Knee",
    ["R15:RightFoot"]     = "R Ankle",
}

-- Props show their own id; limbs use the short form above
local function shortName(part)
    return LIMB_SHORT[part] or part
end

local AXES = { "rx", "ry", "rz", "ox", "oy", "oz" }

local function blankLimb()
    return { rx = 0, ry = 0, rz = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0, oz = 0 }
end

local function blankPose()
    local p = {}
    for _, l in ipairs(POSE_PARTS) do p[l] = blankLimb() end
    return p
end

local function copyPose(src)
    local p = {}
    for _, l in ipairs(POSE_PARTS) do
        local s = src and src[l] or blankLimb()
        p[l] = { rx = s.rx or 0, ry = s.ry or 0, rz = s.rz or 0,
                 ox = s.ox or 0, oy = s.oy or 0, oz = s.oz or 0 }
    end
    -- Carry over tracks belonging to props that aren't registered yet,
    -- otherwise their keyframes would be erased on the first copy.
    if src then
        for l, s in pairs(src) do
            if not p[l] and type(s) == "table" then
                p[l] = { rx = s.rx or 0, ry = s.ry or 0, rz = s.rz or 0,
                         ox = s.ox or 0, oy = s.oy or 0, oz = s.oz or 0 }
            end
        end
    end
    return p
end

local function lerpPose(a, b, alpha)
    local out = {}

    -- union of registered parts and whatever either pose carries
    local keys = {}
    for _, l in ipairs(POSE_PARTS) do keys[l] = true end
    for l, v in pairs(a) do if type(v) == "table" then keys[l] = true end end
    for l, v in pairs(b) do if type(v) == "table" then keys[l] = true end end

    for l in pairs(keys) do
        local pa, pb = a[l] or blankLimb(), b[l] or blankLimb()
        local o = {}
        for _, ax in ipairs(AXES) do
            local va, vb = pa[ax] or 0, pb[ax] or 0
            o[ax] = va + (vb - va) * alpha
        end
        out[l] = o
    end
    return out
end

-- Build a pose table from a sparse {limb = {...}} literal.
-- Tracks for props that aren't registered yet are created on demand,
-- so shipped animations can reference a prop before it exists.
local function pose(tbl)
    local p = blankPose()
    for limb, vals in pairs(tbl) do
        if not p[limb] then p[limb] = blankLimb() end
        for k, v in pairs(vals) do p[limb][k] = v end
    end
    return p
end

        })},
        { t = 0.8, pose = pose({
            ["Torso"]     = { rx = -4, oy = 0.09 },
            ["Left Arm"]  = { rx = -28, rz = 5 },
            ["Right Arm"] = { rx = -32, ry = -10, rz = -8 },
            ["Left Leg"]  = { rx = 34 },
            ["Right Leg"] = { rx = -34 },
        })},
    },
}

Anims["swing"] = {
    loop   = false,
    length = 0.62,
    frames = {
        { t = 0.0,  pose = pose({
            ["Right Arm"] = { rx = -25, ry = -10, rz = -8 },
            ["Left Arm"]  = { rz = 6 },
        })},
        { t = 0.14, pose = pose({
            ["Torso"]     = { ry = -22 },
            ["Head"]      = { ry = 9 },
            ["Right Arm"] = { rx = -150, ry = 35, rz = -40 },
            ["Left Arm"]  = { rx = 12, rz = 14 },
            -- blade cocks back past the wrist on the wind-up
            ["Sword"]     = { rx = -28, rz = 12 },
        })},
        { t = 0.30, pose = pose({
            ["Torso"]     = { ry = 30 },
            ["Head"]      = { ry = -12 },
            ["Right Arm"] = { rx = 45, ry = -40, rz = 30 },
            ["Left Arm"]  = { rx = -18, rz = 2 },
            -- whips forward ahead of the arm, then the arm catches up
            ["Sword"]     = { rx = 34, rz = -16 },
        })},
        { t = 0.46, pose = pose({
            ["Torso"]     = { ry = 10 },
            ["Head"]      = { ry = -4 },
            ["Right Arm"] = { rx = 10, ry = -15, rz = 5 },
            ["Left Arm"]  = { rx = -6, rz = 4 },
            ["Sword"]     = { rx = 10, rz = -5 },
        })},
        { t = 0.62, pose = pose({
            ["Right Arm"] = { rx = -25, ry = -10, rz = -8 },
            ["Left Arm"]  = { rz = 6 },
        })},
    },
}

local ANIM_ORDER = { "idle", "walk", "swing" }


--==================================================
-- SAMPLING
--==================================================

local function sampleAnim(anim, t)
    local f = anim.frames
    if #f == 0 then return blankPose() end
    if #f == 1 then return copyPose(f[1].pose) end

    if anim.loop and anim.length > 0 then
        t = t % anim.length
    end

    if t <= f[1].t then return copyPose(f[1].pose) end
    if t >= f[#f].t then return copyPose(f[#f].pose) end

    for i = 1, #f - 1 do
        local a, b = f[i], f[i + 1]
        if t >= a.t and t < b.t then
            local span  = b.t - a.t
            local alpha = span > 0 and (t - a.t) / span or 0
            -- smoothstep so hand-made keyframes ease in and out. Imported
            -- animations are already resampled with their real easing at
            -- 30 fps, so they interpolate linearly (smoothstep there would
            -- stutter 30 times a second).
            if not anim.linear then
                alpha = alpha * alpha * (3 - 2 * alpha)
            end
            return lerpPose(a.pose, b.pose, alpha)
        end
    end

    return copyPose(f[#f].pose)
end


--==================================================
-- PLAYBACK STATE  (with crossfade)
--==================================================

local playState = {
    serial    = 0,       -- bumps on every switch/restart (music follows it)
    current   = "idle",
    time      = 0,
    blendFrom = nil,     -- pose snapshot taken at the switch
    blendT    = 0,       -- elapsed blend time
    livePose  = blankPose(),
}

local function playAnim(name, restart)
    if not Anims[name] then return end
    if playState.current == name and not restart then return end

    -- Snapshot whatever we're showing right now and fade from it
    playState.blendFrom = copyPose(playState.livePose)
    playState.blendT    = 0
    playState.current   = name
    playState.time      = 0
    playState.serial    = playState.serial + 1
end

-- Which animation fills each role. Any animation can be assigned to
-- any slot, so you never have to edit "idle" itself to change the idle.
local Slots = { idle = "idle", walk = "walk", swing = "swing" }

-- Role the state machine is currently in, tracked separately from the
-- animation name so the same animation can fill several slots safely.
local currentRole = "idle"

-- Editor preview
local previewName = nil    -- animation PLAY is previewing
local previewLoop = true   -- restart it when it finishes

-- Keybind playback
local manualAnim   = nil   -- animation currently held by a keypress
local keyListenFor = nil   -- anim name waiting for the next key (editor)
local onKeyBound   = nil   -- editor callback so the label refreshes

-- Editor override: when set, the rig freezes on this pose
local editPose = nil

local function evaluatePose(dt)
    if editPose then
        playState.livePose = copyPose(editPose)
        return playState.livePose
    end

    local anim = Anims[playState.current] or Anims["idle"]
    playState.time = playState.time + dt * CONFIG.animSpeed
        * (playState.current == "swing" and CONFIG.swingSpeed or 1)

    local target = sampleAnim(anim, playState.time)

    -- Crossfade from the snapshot
    if playState.blendFrom and CONFIG.blendTime > 0 then
        playState.blendT = playState.blendT + dt
        local a = math.clamp(playState.blendT / CONFIG.blendTime, 0, 1)
        a = a * a * (3 - 2 * a)
        target = lerpPose(playState.blendFrom, target, a)
        if a >= 1 then playState.blendFrom = nil end
    end

    playState.livePose = target
    return target
end

local function animFinished()
    local anim = Anims[playState.current]
    if not anim or anim.loop then return false end
    return playState.time >= anim.length
end

-- A one-shot role has to end even if the assigned animation loops,
-- otherwise a looping swing would trap the state machine forever.
local function roleFinished()
    local anim = Anims[playState.current]
    if not anim then return true end
    return playState.time >= anim.length
end

local function cloneAnim(source)
    local frames = {}
    for _, f in ipairs(source.frames) do
        table.insert(frames, { t = f.t, pose = copyPose(f.pose) })
    end
    return {
        loop   = source.loop,
        length = source.length,
        key    = nil,            -- never inherit the bind, it would clash
        linear = source.linear,
        music  = source.music,
        musicVolume = source.musicVolume,
        frames = frames,
    }
end


--==================================================
-- NETLESS
--==================================================

local NETLESS_VELOCITY = 25.01

-- The game's real void line, captured before voidGuard overwrites it
local VOID_Y = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
if VOID_Y ~= VOID_Y then
    -- an earlier run already set it to NaN: use what it saw
    VOID_Y = tonumber(_G.DinoRealDestroyHeight) or -500
    if VOID_Y ~= VOID_Y then VOID_Y = -500 end
end

-- Ownership check without an executor helper: a part you simulate has
-- had nothing received for it from the server, so ReceiveAge is 0.
local function ownsPart(part)
    if type(isnetworkowner) == "function" then
        local ok, r = pcall(isnetworkowner, part)
        if ok then return r end
    end
    local ok, age = pcall(function() return part.ReceiveAge end)
    return ok and age == 0
end
local netlessClaims    = {}
local netlessLastCf    = {}

-- Where the solver will have moved each part by the end of this step.
-- reassert() snaps to this, never backwards to the pre-step pose.
local netlessPredCf    = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
-- Last ownership reading per part, written by the ownership monitor.
-- true = you simulate it, false = the server or another player does.
local PartOwnedState   = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

-- Per-frame root state, set by DriveFrame before any part is driven.
-- driveNetless builds each part's velocity from this.
local DriveCtx = { rootCF = nil, rootVel = Vector3.zero, rootAngVel = Vector3.zero }

-- Each part's previous pose in ROOT space (animation only, no travel).
local netlessLocalPrev = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

-- Last velocity sent per part, for velSmoothing.
local netlessVel       = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
-- Local-only anti-gravity force per part (never replicates).
local antiGravForces   = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

local function applyAntiGravity(handle)
    if not CONFIG.antiGravity then
        local vf = antiGravForces[handle]
        if vf then vf.Force = Vector3.zero end
        return
    end

    local vf = antiGravForces[handle]
    if not (vf and vf.Parent == handle) then
        local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
        att.Name   = "ReanimAG"
        att.Parent = handle

        vf = Instance.new("VectorForce")
        vf.Name                = "ReanimAG"
        vf.Attachment0         = att
        vf.RelativeTo          = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
        vf.ApplyAtCenterOfMass = true
        vf.Force               = Vector3.zero
        vf:SetAttribute("AntiFlingAllowed", true)   -- ours, not injected
        vf.Parent              = handle
        antiGravForces[handle] = vf
    end

    -- Only the assembly root carries the force, or a welded group would
    -- get it once per part. Mass changes during the claim window, so
    -- this is recomputed every frame.
    if handle.AssemblyRootPart == handle then
        vf.Force = Vector3.new(0, handle.AssemblyMass * workspace.Gravity, 0)
    else
        vf.Force = Vector3.zero
    end
end

local function netless(part)
    part.CanCollide = false
    part.Massless   = true
    part.CustomPhysicalProperties = PhysicalProperties.new(0.01, 0, 0, 0, 0)
end

--==================================================
-- DRIVE
--==================================================
--
-- The original netless drive had two problems for a full-body rig.
--
-- 1. THE GROUNDED BRANCH DROPPED OWNERSHIP
--    handle:IsGrounded() returns true for a part resting on the
--    floor. That branch wrote CFrame and nothing else, so the
--    constant velocity that keeps the part client-owned stopped.
--    The server reclaimed it and everyone else saw it at the
--    server's position — which is why your torso was in the right
--    place for you and the wrong place for everyone else. The
--    torso is the part most likely to be grounded, being the
--    largest and sitting on the ground.
--
-- 2. THE LIFT VELOCITY *IS* THE JITTER
--    Writing AssemblyLinearVelocity = (x, 25, z) every frame tells
--    every other client the part is travelling upward at 25
--    studs/s. They interpolate that motion between your CFrame
--    writes, then your next write snaps it back. Moving, then
--    teleporting back, exactly as you described.
--
-- smoothDrive replaces the constant lift with the part's real
-- frame-to-frame velocity. Other clients then interpolate along
-- the path the part is actually taking, so the gaps between
-- writes look like motion instead of correction. Ownership is
-- held by the mass-toggle claim and SimulationRadius instead.
--==================================================

local function driveNetless(handle, newcf, tvel, dt)

    if dt <= 0 then return end
    if not (handle and handle.Parent) then return end

    local timing = os.clock()

    -- never aim a part at or below the void line
    if CONFIG.voidGuard and newcf.Y < VOID_Y + 5 then
        newcf = newcf + Vector3.new(0, VOID_Y + 5 - newcf.Y, 0)
    end

    -- Lost ownership: forget the claim so that the moment it comes back
    -- the part goes through the full claim (strong lift + real mass)
    -- again instead of continuing as if nothing happened.
    if netlessClaims[handle] and not ownsPart(handle) then
        netlessClaims[handle] = nil
    end

    local lastcf = netlessLastCf[handle] or handle.CFrame

    -- true motion of this part, used for both modes
    local vel  = (newcf.Position - lastcf.Position) / dt

    -- ToObjectSpace gives the rotation in lastcf's LOCAL frame, but
    -- AssemblyAngularVelocity is WORLD space. The old code wrote the
    -- local axis straight in, so any limb not facing world-forward got
    -- its spin sent around the wrong axis, and other clients rotated
    -- it the wrong way between updates before snapping back.
    local rel  = lastcf:ToObjectSpace(newcf)
    local a, b = rel:ToAxisAngle()
    local rvel = lastcf:VectorToWorldSpace(a * b) / dt
    if not (rvel.X == rvel.X) then rvel = Vector3.zero end

    ----------------------------------------------------------
    -- Root-anchored velocity.
    --
    -- The delta above is how far the part moved over the LAST
    -- step. On the frame you stop walking the root's velocity is
    -- already ~0 but that delta still says 16 studs/s, so the part
    -- shot a step past your body and got pulled back: the
    -- forward/back jitter, worst exactly when you start or stop.
    --
    -- Instead: travel = the root's own physics velocity (the
    -- same number the root itself sends, and what moves it this
    -- step), plus the spin of the root carrying the part around,
    -- plus the animation's own motion measured in root space.
    -- Body and root now agree on every frame, starts and stops
    -- included.
    ----------------------------------------------------------
    local rootCF = DriveCtx.rootCF
    if rootCF then
        local localNow  = rootCF:ToObjectSpace(newcf)
        local localPrev = netlessLocalPrev[handle]
        netlessLocalPrev[handle] = localNow

        if localPrev then
            local animVel = rootCF:VectorToWorldSpace(
                (localNow.Position - localPrev.Position) / dt)
            local carry = DriveCtx.rootAngVel:Cross(newcf.Position - rootCF.Position)
            local v2 = DriveCtx.rootVel + carry + animVel

            local ra, rb = localPrev:ToObjectSpace(localNow):ToAxisAngle()
            local animSpin = rootCF:VectorToWorldSpace(
                localPrev:VectorToWorldSpace(ra * rb)) / dt
            local w2 = DriveCtx.rootAngVel + animSpin

            if v2.X == v2.X and v2.Y == v2.Y and v2.Z == v2.Z then vel = v2 end
            if w2.X == w2.X and w2.Y == w2.Y and w2.Z == w2.Z then rvel = w2 end
        end
    end

    netlessLastCf[handle] = newcf

    ----------------------------------------------------------
    -- Ownership claim
    --
    -- Real mass for the first stretch makes the server hand us
    -- network ownership; massless afterwards keeps the part from
    -- disturbing anything. This runs in BOTH branches now, so a
    -- grounded part never silently loses its claim.
    ----------------------------------------------------------

    local claimtime = netlessClaims[handle]

    if not claimtime then
        netlessClaims[handle] = timing
        claimtime = timing
    end

    if timing - claimtime < 5.67 then
        handle.Massless = false
        handle.CustomPhysicalProperties = nil
    else
        handle.Massless = true
        handle.CustomPhysicalProperties =
            PhysicalProperties.new(0.01, 0, 0, 0, 0)
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------
    -- Motion
    ----------------------------------------------------------

    if CONFIG.smoothDrive then

        -- Velocity that matches where the part is actually going.
        -- Clamped so a teleport-sized delta does not turn into a
        -- launch on someone else's screen.
        if not (vel.X == vel.X) then vel = Vector3.zero end

        if vel.Magnitude > CONFIG.maxDriveSpeed then
            vel = vel.Unit * CONFIG.maxDriveSpeed
        end

        if rvel.Magnitude > CONFIG.maxDriveSpin then
            rvel = rvel.Unit * CONFIG.maxDriveSpin
        end

        -- Frame-time noise filter
        local k = math.clamp(CONFIG.velSmoothing or 0, 0, 0.9)
        local prev = netlessVel[handle]
        if prev and k > 0 then
            vel = prev:Lerp(vel, 1 - k)
        end
        netlessVel[handle] = vel

        -- What gets SENT. With netlessLift the vertical part is held
        -- at the lift speed (stronger for the first half second of a
        -- claim). `vel` stays the true motion and drives the prediction
        -- below, so the reassert pins the part on the pose and nothing
        -- visibly rises.
        local sendVel = vel
        if CONFIG.netlessLift then
            local lift = NETLESS_VELOCITY + (math.sin(timing * 0.5) + 1) / 2
            if timing - claimtime < 0.51 then lift = lift * 2 end
            sendVel = Vector3.new(vel.X, math.max(vel.Y, lift), vel.Z)
        end

        handle.CFrame                  = newcf
        handle.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = sendVel
        handle.AssemblyAngularVelocity = rvel
        applyAntiGravity(handle)

        -- Where this step will leave the part. The solver moves it by
        -- vel*dt and spins it by rvel*dt; reassert() holds it THERE.
        local pred = newcf + vel * dt
        local spin = rvel.Magnitude * dt
        if spin > 1e-6 then
            pred = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(rvel.Unit, spin) * (pred - pred.Position) + pred.Position
        end
        netlessPredCf[handle] = pred

    else

        -- Original behaviour, kept for comparison.
        local netlessv = NETLESS_VELOCITY + (math.sin(timing * 0.5) + 1) / 2

        local idleoff = Vector3.new(
            math.sin(timing * 14),
            math.sin(timing * 15 + 1.0472),
            math.sin(timing * 16 + 2.0944)) * 0.001

        tvel = tvel or Vector3.zero
        local lift = vel + tvel
        lift = lift * Vector3.new(1, 0, 1)
        if lift.Magnitude > netlessv then lift = lift.Unit * netlessv end

        handle.CFrame                  = newcf
        handle.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.new(lift.X, netlessv, lift.Z)
        handle.AssemblyAngularVelocity = rvel + idleoff
        netlessPredCf[handle] = newcf
    end
end


-- Re-assert CFrame only, with no physics writes.
--
-- Called after the solver has run. It used to snap each part BACK to
-- the pose from before the step, while its velocity said it had moved
-- on. Whatever the engine sent to other players that frame was then a
-- position from one moment paired with a velocity from the next,
-- which they saw as a constant fine shake. It also left your body a
-- step behind your root.
--
-- Now it holds each part where the step was supposed to leave it.
-- With gravity cancelled that is exactly where the solver already
-- put it, so this only corrects real drift.
local function reassert(handle)
    if not (handle and handle.Parent) then return end
    local cf = netlessPredCf[handle] or netlessLastCf[handle]
    if cf then
        handle.CFrame = cf
    end
end


--==================================================
-- JOINT STRIPPING
--==================================================

local function isJoint(o)
    return o:IsA("JointInstance") or o:IsA("WeldConstraint")
end

local function stripAll(handle, char)
    for _, j in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
        if isJoint(j) then j:Destroy() end
    end
    local acc = handle.Parent
    if acc and acc:IsA("Accessory") then
        for _, j in ipairs(acc:GetChildren()) do
            if isJoint(j) then j:Destroy() end
        end
    end
    if not char then return end
    for _, d in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
        if isJoint(d) and (d.Part0 == handle or d.Part1 == handle) then
            d:Destroy()
        end
    end
end


--==================================================
-- NO-RESPAWN
--==================================================

--==================================================
-- SHARED STATE
--==================================================

local corpseParts = {}
local driverChar  = nil

-- World position of the head exactly as it is DRAWN this frame (the
-- pose target the PostSimulation reassert snaps it to). The render-step
-- camera pass converts it against the root's CURRENT CFrame, so the
-- camera lands on the visible head, not on where the root moved to.
local camHeadPos = nil

-- The ORIGINAL HumanoidRootPart (now inside the Corpse model). It is the
-- only one the server knows about, so it is what other players see as
-- "you": TextChatService hangs chat bubbles on it (BubbleChatConfiguration
-- .AdorneeName defaults to "HumanoidRootPart"), and StreamingEnabled
-- games stream your character around it. It used to be left loose after
-- BreakJoints, so it dropped or fell out of the world and took your
-- bubbles with it. It is now driven onto your driver root every frame.
local corpseRoot = nil
-- Head position at rest relative to the root. R6: the camera's own
-- subject height. R15: measured from your rig at the split.
local CAM_HEAD_REST = Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)

-- Worn accessories on the corpse (hats, hair, faces...).
-- BreakJoints destroys their AccessoryWeld along with the limb joints,
-- so each one's offset is captured first and re-applied every frame.
local hatData = {}   -- { handle = BasePart, part = BasePart, rel = CFrame }

-- New accessories that arrive after the split register themselves
local autoAddNew = true

-- Rig parts currently bound, keyed by limb name.
local rigHandles = {}
-- Local-only driver clones made at the split. They carry copies of the
-- rig accessories that nobody else can see, so rig lookups skip them.
local localClone = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
-- Each limb's POSE CFrame from the last frame. Hats and props follow
-- this instead of the physical limb, because the physical limb may be
-- parked under the floor.
local lastLimbPose = {}
-- Fires when a rig part is bound or lost, so the RIG tab repaints.
local onRigChanged = nil

-- One spare per limb, keyed by limb name. Driven tucked in the torso.
local rigSpares = {}
local lastAutoGh = -math.huge

-- Every worn hat gets a watcher: if it leaves your character or the game,
-- you're told when, where and the most likely reason, in F9 and as a
-- notification, so a vanishing hat can be pinned down instead of guessed.
local watchedHats = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local lastHatGh = -math.huge
local reanimStartedAt = os.clock()

local function hatNotify(text)
    pcall(function()
        StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", { Title = "Reanimate", Text = text, Duration = 6 })
    end)
end

local function watchHat(h, char)
    if not h or watchedHats[h] then return end
    watchedHats[h] = true
    local acc = h.Parent
    local accName = acc and acc.Name or h.Name
    local born = os.clock()

    -- accessory pulled out of your character but still in the world:
    -- keep driving it, just report it
    if acc and acc:IsA("Accessory") then
        acc.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
            if not acc.Parent then return end
            if char and not acc:IsDescendantOf(char) and acc:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                warn(string.format("[Hat] %s was moved out of your character by the game (now in %s); still driving it",
                    accName, acc.Parent:GetFullName()))
            end
        end)
    end

    local conn
    conn = h.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
        if h:IsDescendantOf(game) then return end
        conn:Disconnect()

        local y     = h.Position.Y
        local owned = PartOwnedState[h]
        local cause
        if y < VOID_Y + 60 then
            cause = "it fell into the void: someone else was simulating it"
        elseif owned == false then
            cause = "you did NOT own it: on the server it was dropping while your screen still held it in place (Y here is your local copy), so it most likely hit the void there"
        elseif owned == true then
            cause = "you owned it the whole time, so physics didn't take it: the game/server deleted it (a game script, a chat command like -pd, or another player's tool)"
        else
            cause = "ownership never got checked, so it can't be told apart; the next run will know"
        end
        local msg = string.format("%s disappeared %.1fs after it was captured (%.0fs into the reanimate) · Y=%.0f · owner at last check: %s · %s",
            accName, os.clock() - born, os.clock() - reanimStartedAt, y,
            owned == nil and "unknown" or (owned and "you" or "NOT you"), cause)
        warn("[Hat] " .. msg)
        hatNotify(accName .. " disappeared · check F9 for why")

        -- bring it back if you listed its id
        if #KEEP_HAT_IDS > 0 and activeChar and activeChar.Parent and Ext.alive() then
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - lastHatGh >= KEEP_HAT_COOLDOWN then
                lastHatGh = now
                warn("[Hat] re-requesting: -gh " .. table.concat(KEEP_HAT_IDS, " "))
                send("-gh " .. table.concat(KEEP_HAT_IDS, " "))
            end
        end

        for i = #hatData, 1, -1 do
            if hatData[i].handle == h then table.remove(hatData, i) end
        end
    end)
end

-- Which R6 limb a corpse part is, so anything worn on it can follow the
-- POSE even after the real limb has been parked or lost.
local function limbNameOfPart(part)
    for name, lp_ in pairs(corpseParts) do
        if lp_ == part then return name end
    end
    return nil
end

local function isRigHandle(h)
    if not h then return false end
    for _, rh in pairs(rigHandles) do
        if rh == h then return true end
    end
    for _, sh in pairs(rigSpares) do
        if sh == h then return true end
    end
    return false
end

-- Default grip per attach point, so a freshly added prop lands
-- somewhere sane instead of inside the torso.
local DEFAULT_GRIP = {
    ["Right Arm"] = { gx = 0, gy = -1.1, gz = 0,   grx = -90, gry = 0, grz = 0 },
    ["Left Arm"]  = { gx = 0, gy = -1.1, gz = 0,   grx = -90, gry = 0, grz = 0 },
    ["Head"]      = { gx = 0, gy = 0.6,  gz = 0,   grx = 0,   gry = 0, grz = 0 },
    ["Torso"]     = { gx = 0, gy = 0,    gz = -0.6, grx = 0,  gry = 0, grz = 0 },
    ["Left Leg"]  = { gx = 0, gy = -0.6, gz = 0,   grx = 0,   gry = 0, grz = 0 },
    ["Right Leg"] = { gx = 0, gy = -0.6, gz = 0,   grx = 0,   gry = 0, grz = 0 },
}

local onPropsChanged = nil   -- editor hook so the UI repaints

-- Every accessory Handle carries one Attachment whose name says which
-- body part it belongs on. This is how Roblox itself places them.
local ATTACH_MAP = {
    HatAttachment           = "Head",   HairAttachment         = "Head",
    FaceFrontAttachment     = "Head",   FaceCenterAttachment   = "Head",
    NeckAttachment          = "Torso",  BodyFrontAttachment    = "Torso",
    BodyBackAttachment      = "Torso",  LeftCollarAttachment   = "Torso",
    RightCollarAttachment   = "Torso",  WaistFrontAttachment   = "Torso",
    WaistCenterAttachment   = "Torso",  WaistBackAttachment    = "Torso",
    LeftShoulderAttachment  = "Left Arm",  RightShoulderAttachment = "Right Arm",
    LeftGripAttachment      = "Left Arm",  RightGripAttachment     = "Right Arm",
    LeftFootAttachment      = "Left Leg",  RightFootAttachment     = "Right Leg",
}

-- Returns attach limb + the limb-space CFrame the handle should rest at.
-- Uses the same maths Roblox uses when it equips an accessory:
--     handle = limbAttachment * handleAttachment:Inverse()
-- so the result is correct regardless of where the part currently is.
local function resolveAttach(acc, handle)
    local hAtt
    for _, a in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
        if a:IsA("Attachment") and ATTACH_MAP[a.Name] then hAtt = a break end
    end

    -- R15: the attachment lives on a specific R15 part (RightGripAttachment
    -- on RightHand, HatAttachment on Head ...). Ride that part.
    if IS_R15 and hAtt then
        for name, part in pairs(corpseParts) do
            local lAtt = part:FindFirstChild(hAtt.Name)
            if lAtt and lAtt:IsA("Attachment") then
                return name, lAtt.CFrame * hAtt.CFrame:Inverse()
            end
        end
    end

    local attach = hAtt and ATTACH_MAP[hAtt.Name] or "Head"
    local limb   = corpseParts[attach]

    if hAtt and limb then
        local lAtt = limb:FindFirstChild(hAtt.Name)
        if lAtt and lAtt:IsA("Attachment") then
            return attach, lAtt.CFrame * hAtt.CFrame:Inverse()
        end
    end

    -- no matching attachment: keep it where it is right now
    if limb then
        local base = lastLimbPose[attach] or limb.CFrame
        return attach, base:ToObjectSpace(handle.CFrame)
    end
    return attach, nil
end

function addProp(acc, handle, char, attach, forcedId, restCF)
    if not (acc and handle) then return nil end

    -- unique track id; forcedId lets the auto-claimed sword land on
    -- "Sword" so the shipped swing keyframes match it
    local base = forcedId
    if not base then
        base = acc.Name:gsub("^Accessory %((.*)%)$", "%1")
        if base == "" then base = acc.Name end
    end
    local id, n = base, 1
    while findProp(id) do n = n + 1 id = base .. " " .. n end

    stripAll(handle, char)
    -- reset the netless claim so the prop loop starts clean instead of
    -- inheriting whatever the hat follow left behind
    netlessClaims[handle] = nil
    netlessLastCf[handle] = nil
    netlessPredCf[handle] = nil
    netlessVel[handle]    = nil
    netlessLocalPrev[handle] = nil
    netless(handle)
    handle.Transparency = 0
    handle.CanCollide   = false
    handle.Anchored     = false
    if char and acc.Parent ~= char then acc.Parent = char end

    attach = attach or "Right Arm"
    local g = DEFAULT_GRIP[attach] or DEFAULT_GRIP["Right Arm"]

    -- Offset each additional prop on the same limb, otherwise they stack
    -- in exactly the same spot and it looks like only one was added.
    local sameLimb = 0
    for _, other in ipairs(Props) do
        if other.attach == attach then sameLimb += 1 end
    end

    local pr
    if restCF then
        -- derived from the accessory's own attachment: lands exactly
        -- where it would sit if Roblox equipped it
        local rx, ry, rz = restCF:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
        pr = {
            id = id, accName = acc.Name, handle = handle, attach = attach,
            gx = restCF.X, gy = restCF.Y, gz = restCF.Z,
            grx = math.deg(rx), gry = math.deg(ry), grz = math.deg(rz),
        }
    else
        pr = {
            id = id, accName = acc.Name, handle = handle, attach = attach,
            gx = g.gx + sameLimb * 0.6, gy = g.gy, gz = g.gz,
            grx = g.grx, gry = g.gry, grz = g.grz,
        }
    end
    table.insert(Props, pr)
    rebuildPoseParts()

    -- give every existing keyframe a blank track for the new prop
    for _, anim in pairs(Anims) do
        for _, f in ipairs(anim.frames) do
            if not f.pose[id] then f.pose[id] = blankLimb() end
        end
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Disappearance forensics. If the handle leaves the game, say why
    -- and drop the dead track so the editor doesn't keep a ghost.
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    local born = os.clock()
    local watch
    watch = handle.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
        if handle:IsDescendantOf(game) then return end
        watch:Disconnect()

        local y     = handle.Position.Y
        local voidY = VOID_Y
        local owned = PartOwnedState[handle]

        local cause
        if y < voidY + 60 then
            cause = "FELL INTO THE VOID: someone other than you was simulating it"
        elseif owned == false then
            cause = "removed while the server or another player owned it"
        else
            cause = "removed by the game itself, not a physics loss"
        end

        warn(string.format(
            "[Prop] '%s' disappeared after %.1fs · Y=%.0f (void %.0f) · owner at last check: %s · %s",
            pr.id, os.clock() - born, y, voidY,
            owned == nil and "unknown" or (owned and "you" or "NOT you"),
            cause))

        task.defer(function()
            local _, i = findProp(pr.id)
            if i and Props[i] == pr then
                table.remove(Props, i)
                rebuildPoseParts()
                if onPropsChanged then pcall(onPropsChanged) end
            end
        end)
    end)

    if onPropsChanged then pcall(onPropsChanged) end
    warn("[Prop] added: " .. id .. "  →  " .. attach)
    return pr
end

function removeProp(id)
    local pr, i = findProp(id)
    if not pr then return end

    -- Keep it on the body instead of letting it drop: freeze its current
    -- offset from the limb it was riding and let the hat follow take over.
    local h    = pr.handle
    local limb = corpseParts[pr.attach] or corpseParts["Head"]
    if h and h.Parent and limb then
        local limbName = corpseParts[pr.attach] and pr.attach or "Head"
        local base = lastLimbPose[limbName] or limb.CFrame
        table.insert(hatData, {
            handle = h,
            part   = limb,
            limb   = limbName,
            rel    = base:ToObjectSpace(h.CFrame),
        })
    end

    table.remove(Props, i)
    rebuildPoseParts()
    if onPropsChanged then pcall(onPropsChanged) end
end

--==================================================
-- RIG BINDING
--==================================================

-- Resolve whatever carries the rig mesh to the BasePart that gets driven.
local function rigHandleOf(inst)
    if not inst then return nil end
    if inst:IsA("BasePart") then return inst end
    if inst:IsA("Accessory") or inst:IsA("Accoutrement") then
        local h = inst:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        if h and h:IsA("BasePart") then return h end
        return inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    end
    if inst:IsA("Model") then
        return inst.PrimaryPart or inst:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
    end
    return nil
end

-- First real (server) instance named `name` under root.
local function findRigInstance(root, name)
    if not root then return nil end
    for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        if d.Name == name and not localClone[d] then
            local h = rigHandleOf(d)
            if h and not localClone[h] then return d, h end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local bindRigPart   -- forward: the loss handler re-binds spares

-- Shared prep for anything the rig drives: cut its welds, fresh claim
local function prepRigHandle(h)
    stripAll(h, activeChar)
    netlessClaims[h]    = nil
    netlessLastCf[h]    = nil
    netlessPredCf[h]    = nil
    netlessVel[h]       = nil
    netlessLocalPrev[h] = nil
    netless(h)
    h.CanCollide = false
    h.Anchored   = false
    for i = #hatData, 1, -1 do
        if hatData[i].handle == h then table.remove(hatData, i) end
    end
    local hadProp = false
    for i = #Props, 1, -1 do
        if Props[i].handle == h then table.remove(Props, i) hadProp = true end
    end
    if hadProp then
        rebuildPoseParts()
        if onPropsChanged then pcall(onPropsChanged) end
    end
end

local function requestReplacement(reason)
    if not (RIG.enabled and RIG.autoRegh) then return end
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - lastAutoGh < RIG.reghCooldown then return end
    lastAutoGh = now
    warn("[Rig] re-sending -gh (" .. reason .. ")")
    send("-gh " .. GH_IDS)
end

-- Spare for a limb whose part is already bound
local function addSpare(rp, h)
    if not (rp and h) then return false end
    local cur = rigSpares[rp.limb]
    if cur and cur.Parent then return false end
    prepRigHandle(h)
    rigSpares[rp.limb] = h
    local watch
    watch = h.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
        if h:IsDescendantOf(game) then return end
        watch:Disconnect()
        if rigSpares[rp.limb] == h then rigSpares[rp.limb] = nil end
        if onRigChanged then pcall(onRigChanged) end
    end)
    warn("[Rig] spare ready for " .. rp.limb)
    if onRigChanged then pcall(onRigChanged) end
    return true
end

-- Called when a bound rig part leaves the game
local function onRigLost(rp)
    local spare = rigSpares[rp.limb]
    if spare and spare.Parent then
        rigSpares[rp.limb] = nil
        bindRigPart(rp, spare)
        warn("[Rig] spare took over " .. rp.limb)
        -- refill the spare in the background
        requestReplacement("refilling " .. rp.limb .. " spare")
        return
    end
    requestReplacement(rp.limb .. " lost")
end

bindRigPart = function(rp, h)
    if not (rp and h) then return end

    stripAll(h, activeChar)

    -- fresh ownership claim for the new driver
    netlessClaims[h]    = nil
    netlessLastCf[h]    = nil
    netlessPredCf[h]    = nil
    netlessVel[h]       = nil
    netlessLocalPrev[h] = nil
    netless(h)
    h.CanCollide = false
    h.Anchored   = false

    -- a rig part is never a hat or a prop as well; two drivers on one
    -- part fight over its claim
    for i = #hatData, 1, -1 do
        if hatData[i].handle == h then table.remove(hatData, i) end
    end
    local hadProp = false
    for i = #Props, 1, -1 do
        if Props[i].handle == h then table.remove(Props, i) hadProp = true end
    end
    if hadProp then
        rebuildPoseParts()
        if onPropsChanged then pcall(onPropsChanged) end
    end

    rigHandles[rp.limb] = h

    -- Report it if the game ever removes the part
    local born = os.clock()
    local watch
    watch = h.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
        if h:IsDescendantOf(game) then return end
        watch:Disconnect()
        local wasBound = rigHandles[rp.limb] == h
        if wasBound then rigHandles[rp.limb] = nil end
        local owned = PartOwnedState[h]
        warn(string.format(
            "[Rig] %s (%s) disappeared after %.1fs · Y=%.0f · owner at last check: %s",
            rp.limb, rp.name, os.clock() - born, h.Position.Y,
            owned == nil and "unknown" or (owned and "you" or "NOT you")))
        if wasBound and activeChar and activeChar.Parent then
            task.defer(onRigLost, rp)
        end
        if onRigChanged then pcall(onRigChanged) end
    end)

    warn("[Rig] bound " .. rp.name .. "  →  " .. rp.limb)
    if onRigChanged then pcall(onRigChanged) end
end

-- Binds every rig part found under root that isn't bound yet.
-- Returns found count and a list of missing names.
local function scanRig(root)
    local found, missing = 0, {}
    for _, rp in ipairs(RIG.parts) do
        local cur = rigHandles[rp.limb]
        if not (cur and cur.Parent) then
            rigHandles[rp.limb] = nil
            local _, h = findRigInstance(root, rp.name)
            if h then bindRigPart(rp, h) end
        end
        if rigHandles[rp.limb] then
            found += 1
        else
            table.insert(missing, rp.name)
        end
    end
    return found, missing
end

-- Pre-split check: how many rig parts are present on the character.
local function countRigParts(root)
    local found, missing = 0, {}
    for _, rp in ipairs(RIG.parts) do
        if findRigInstance(root, rp.name) then
            found += 1
        else
            table.insert(missing, rp.name)
        end
    end
    return found, missing
end

-- Client-side hide for a real limb (does not replicate; the REAL BODY
-- mode is what hides it from other players).
local function hideRealLocal(part)
    if part.Transparency ~= 1 then part.Transparency = 1 end
    for _, d in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
        if d:IsA("Decal") and d.Transparency ~= 1 then d.Transparency = 1 end
    end
end

local function respawnrequest()
    local ccfr, ch = ws.CurrentCamera.CFrame, lp.Character
    lp.Character = nil
    lp.Character = ch
    local con
    con = ws.CurrentCamera.Changed:Connect(function(prop)
        if prop ~= "Parent" and prop ~= "CFrame" then return end
        ws.CurrentCamera.CFrame = ccfr
        con:Disconnect()
    end)
end


--==================================================
-- SETUP  (Zeon split + perma-death)
--==================================================

local function setup(c)
    if not (c and c.Parent) then return end
    activeChar, driverChar = c, c
    corpseParts   = {}
    hatData       = {}
    Props         = {}
    camHeadPos    = nil
    corpseRoot    = nil
    rigHandles    = {}
    rigSpares     = {}
    lastLimbPose  = {}
    reanimStartedAt = os.clock()
    rebuildPoseParts()

    local function antiragdoll(v)
        if v:IsA("HingeConstraint") or v:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") then
            v.Parent = nil
        end
    end
    for _, v in c:GetDescendants() do antiragdoll(v) end
    c.DescendantAdded:Connect(antiragdoll)

    -- body type, read before anything is moved
    do
        local h0 = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        IS_R15 = (h0 and h0.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
            or (c:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") ~= nil)
        R15Joints, R15Order = {}, {}
        CAM_HEAD_REST = Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
        warn("[Reanim] body type: " .. (IS_R15 and "R15" or "R6"))
    end

    c.Archivable = true
    local cl = c:Clone()
    cl.Parent = ws

    local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        pcall(function()
            for _, track in hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() do track:Stop() end
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
        end)
    end

    local model = Instance.new("Model", c)
    model.Name  = "Corpse"
    for _, v in c:GetChildren() do
        if v ~= model then v.Parent = model end
    end
    for _, v in model:GetDescendants() do
        if v:IsA("LocalScript") then v.Disabled = true end
        if v:IsA("BasePart")    then v.CanCollide = false end
    end

    local hum0 = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum0 then
        pcall(function()
            hum0.BreakJointsOnDeath = false
            hum0.Health             = 0
        end)
        -- R15: scaling changes make the Humanoid rebuild its joints
        pcall(function() hum0.AutomaticScalingEnabled = false end)
        task.spawn(function()
            while model and model.Parent do
                if hum0 and hum0.Parent and hum0.Health > 0 then
                    pcall(function() hum0.Health = 0 end)
                end
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        end)
    end

    for _, n in ipairs(IS_R15 and R15_PARTS or LIMBS) do
        local p = model:FindFirstChild(n)
        if p and p:IsA("BasePart") then
            netless(p)
            corpseParts[n] = p
        end
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- R15: capture every joint's offsets BEFORE they break, then work
    -- out a parent-first order for the forward kinematics.
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    if IS_R15 then
        local source = {}

        -- 1. Motor6D (classic R15)
        for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("Motor6D") and d.Part0 and d.Part1 and R15_GROUP[d.Part1.Name] then
                R15Joints[d.Part1.Name] = { parent = d.Part0.Name, c0 = d.C0, c1 = d.C1 }
                source[d.Part1.Name] = "Motor6D"
            end
        end

        -- 2. AnimationConstraint (avatar joint upgrade)
        for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("AnimationConstraint") and d.Attachment0 and d.Attachment1 then
                local a0, a1 = d.Attachment0, d.Attachment1
                local p0, p1 = a0.Parent, a1.Parent
                if p0 and p1 and p0:IsA("BasePart") and p1:IsA("BasePart")
                    and R15_GROUP[p1.Name] and not R15Joints[p1.Name] then
                    R15Joints[p1.Name] = { parent = p0.Name, c0 = a0.CFrame, c1 = a1.CFrame }
                    source[p1.Name] = "AnimationConstraint"
                end
            end
        end

        -- 3. Rig attachments (always present on R15 parts)
        for child, def in pairs(R15_RIG) do
            if not R15Joints[child] then
                local cp = model:FindFirstChild(child)
                local pp = model:FindFirstChild(def[1])
                local ca = cp and cp:FindFirstChild(def[2])
                local pa = pp and pp:FindFirstChild(def[2])
                if ca and pa and ca:IsA("Attachment") and pa:IsA("Attachment") then
                    R15Joints[child] = { parent = def[1], c0 = pa.CFrame, c1 = ca.CFrame }
                    source[child] = "attachments"
                end
            end
        end

        local counts = {}
        for _, src in pairs(source) do counts[src] = (counts[src] or 0) + 1 end
        local parts = {}
        for src, n in pairs(counts) do table.insert(parts, n .. " " .. src) end
        warn("[Reanim] R15 joint sources: " .. (#parts > 0 and table.concat(parts, ", ") or "NONE"))
        local placed = { HumanoidRootPart = true }
        for _ = 1, #R15_PARTS do
            local progress = false
            for _, n in ipairs(R15_PARTS) do
                local j = R15Joints[n]
                if j and not placed[n] and placed[j.parent] then
                    placed[n] = true
                    table.insert(R15Order, n)
                    progress = true
                end
            end
            if not progress then break end
        end

        -- rest head height relative to the root, for the camera
        local cfs = { HumanoidRootPart = CFrame.identity }
        for _, n in ipairs(R15Order) do
            local j = R15Joints[n]
            cfs[n] = cfs[j.parent] * j.c0 * j.c1:Inverse()
        end
        if cfs.Head then CAM_HEAD_REST = cfs.Head.Position end

        local missing = {}
        for _, n in ipairs(R15_PARTS) do
            if not R15Joints[n] then table.insert(missing, n) end
        end
        warn(string.format("[Reanim] R15 joints captured: %d/%d%s", #R15Order, #R15_PARTS,
            #missing > 0 and (" · missing: " .. table.concat(missing, ", ")) or ""))
    end

    -- editor tracks for this body type
    rebuildPoseParts()
    if IS_R15 then
        for _, anim in pairs(Anims) do
            for _, f in ipairs(anim.frames) do
                for _, k in ipairs(R15_EDIT) do
                    if not f.pose[k] then f.pose[k] = blankLimb() end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if onPropsChanged then task.defer(function() pcall(onPropsChanged) end) end

    -- Real root: kept separate from corpseParts so the 6-limb status
    -- count and the pose loop stay unchanged.
    do
        local r = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if r and r:IsA("BasePart") then
            netless(r)
            r.CanCollide   = false
            r.Transparency = 1
            corpseRoot = r
        end
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Bind the rig BEFORE hats are captured, so no rig part is ever
    -- mistaken for a hat and driven twice.
    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    if RIG.enabled then
        local found, missing = scanRig(model)
        if found == #RIG.parts then
            warn("[Rig] all " .. found .. " rig parts bound")
        else
            warn(string.format("[Rig] %d/%d rig parts bound · missing: %s · they bind automatically if they arrive later, or press RESCAN in the RIG tab",
                found, #RIG.parts, table.concat(missing, ", ")))
        end
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Capture worn accessories BEFORE the joints break.
    -- Whatever body part each handle is welded to becomes its anchor;
    -- rel is the offset that keeps it sitting correctly afterwards.
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    local function weldTargetOf(handle, acc)
        -- joint inside the accessory
        for _, d in ipairs(acc:GetDescendants()) do
            if (d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("Motor6D")) and d.Part0 and d.Part1 then
                if d.Part0 == handle and d.Part1:IsA("BasePart") then return d.Part1 end
                if d.Part1 == handle and d.Part0:IsA("BasePart") then return d.Part0 end
            end
        end
        -- joint parked elsewhere in the model (Roblox often puts it on the Head)
        for _, d in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            if (d:IsA("Weld") or d:IsA("Motor6D") or d:IsA("WeldConstraint"))
                and d.Part0 and d.Part1 then
                if d.Part0 == handle and d.Part1:IsA("BasePart") then return d.Part1 end
                if d.Part1 == handle and d.Part0:IsA("BasePart") then return d.Part0 end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function captureHats()
        for _, acc in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
            if acc:IsA("Accessory") then
                local h = acc:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                    or acc:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")

                local already = false
                for _, hd in ipairs(hatData) do
                    if hd.handle == h then already = true break end
                end

                if h and not already and not isRigHandle(h) then
                    -- fall back to the Head: correct for hats, hair and faces
                    local target = weldTargetOf(h, acc) or corpseParts["Head"]

                    if target and target:IsA("BasePart") then
                        netless(h)
                        h.CanCollide = false
                        table.insert(hatData, {
                            handle = h,
                            part   = target,
                            limb   = limbNameOfPart(target),
                            rel    = target.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(h.CFrame),
                        })
                        watchHat(h, c)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    captureHats()

    task.delay(0.12, function()
        if model and model.Parent then
            pcall(model.BreakJoints, model)

            -- BreakJoints misses constraint-based joints (newer R15
            -- avatars). Remove every joint attached to a body part.
            local bodySet = {}
            for _, part in pairs(corpseParts) do bodySet[part] = true end
            if corpseRoot then bodySet[corpseRoot] = true end
            local removed = 0
            for _, v in model:GetDescendants() do
                if isBodyJoint(v) then
                    local hit = false
                    pcall(function()
                        if v:IsA("JointInstance") or v:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
                            hit = bodySet[v.Part0] or bodySet[v.Part1]
                        else
                            local a0, a1 = v.Attachment0, v.Attachment1
                            hit = (a0 and bodySet[a0.Parent]) or (a1 and bodySet[a1.Parent])
                        end
                    end)
                    if hit then
                        pcall(v.Destroy, v)
                        removed += 1
                    end
                end
            end
            if removed > 0 then
                warn("[Reanim] removed " .. removed .. " extra body joints BreakJoints left behind")
            end

            for _, v in model:GetDescendants() do
                if v:IsA("BasePart") then
                    v.Anchored   = false
                    v.CanCollide = false
                end
            end

            -- Keep them apart. GetJoints() finds a joint no matter where
            -- it is parented, and the sweep catches anything the game or
            -- the Humanoid builds again later (R15 Humanoids can rebuild
            -- their rig joints on their own).
            local function stripBodyJoints()
                local found = {}
                for part in pairs(bodySet) do
                    if part.Parent then
                        local ok, joints = pcall(function() return part:GetJoints() end)
                        if ok and type(joints) == "table" then
                            for _, j in ipairs(joints) do
                                if j.Name ~= "ReanimAG" and isBodyJoint(j) then
                                    local other
                                    pcall(function()
                                        if j:IsA("JointInstance") or j:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
                                            other = (j.Part0 == part) and j.Part1 or j.Part0
                                        else
                                            local a0, a1 = j.Attachment0, j.Attachment1
                                            local p0 = a0 and a0.Parent
                                            local p1 = a1 and a1.Parent
                                            other = (p0 == part) and p1 or p0
                                        end
                                    end)
                                    if other and bodySet[other] then
                                        local where = "?"
                                        pcall(function() where = j:GetFullName() end)
                                        table.insert(found, j.ClassName .. " (" .. where .. ")")
                                        pcall(j.Destroy, j)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return found
            end

            local function stuckParts()
                local stuck = {}
                for name, part in pairs(corpseParts) do
                    local ok, connected = pcall(function() return part:GetConnectedParts(false) end)
                    if ok and type(connected) == "table" then
                        for _, other in ipairs(connected) do
                            if other ~= part and bodySet[other] then
                                table.insert(stuck, name)
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return stuck
            end

            local first = stripBodyJoints()
            if #first > 0 then
                warn(string.format("[Reanim] cut %d joints BreakJoints missed, e.g. %s",
                    #first, table.concat(first, " | ", 1, math.min(3, #first))))
            end

            -- new joints appearing inside the corpse: cut them right away
            model.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(v)
                if not isBodyJoint(v) or v.Name == "ReanimAG" then return end
                task.defer(function()
                    local hit = false
                    pcall(function()
                        if v:IsA("JointInstance") or v:IsA("WeldConstraint") then
                            hit = bodySet[v.Part0] and bodySet[v.Part1]
                        else
                            local a0, a1 = v.Attachment0, v.Attachment1
                            hit = a0 and a1 and bodySet[a0.Parent] and bodySet[a1.Parent]
                        end
                    end)
                    if hit then pcall(v.Destroy, v) end
                end)
            end)

            task.spawn(function()
                local rebuilt, reported = 0, false
                local t0 = os.clock()
                while model and model.Parent do
                    task.wait((os.clock() - t0 < 5) and 0.1 or 0.5)
                    local again = stripBodyJoints()
                    if #again > 0 then
                        rebuilt += #again
                        if not reported then
                            reported = true
                            warn("[Reanim] the game rebuilt body joints, cutting them as they come back: "
                                .. table.concat(again, " | ", 1, math.min(3, #again)))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)

            task.delay(0.3, function()
                local stuck = stuckParts()
                if #stuck == 0 then
                    warn("[Reanim] all body parts free")
                    return
                end
                -- still stuck: say exactly what is holding the first one
                local holder = {}
                local p0 = corpseParts[stuck[1]]
                pcall(function()
                    for _, j in ipairs(p0:GetJoints()) do
                        table.insert(holder, j.ClassName .. " " .. j:GetFullName())
                    end
                end)
