            if id == "PIC" then
                mime = (body:sub(2, 4):upper() == "PNG") and "image/png" or "image/jpeg"
                i = 5
            else
                local z = body:find("\0", 2, true)
                if z then
                    mime = body:sub(2, z - 1):lower()
                    i = z + 1
                end
            end
            -- skip the picture-type byte
            i += 1
            if enc == 1 or enc == 2 then
                local j = i
                while j + 1 <= #body do
                    if body:byte(j) == 0 and body:byte(j + 1) == 0 then break end
                    j += 2
                end
                i = j + 2
            else
                local z = body:find("\0", i, true)
                i = (z or (i - 1)) + 1
            end
            local img = body:sub(i)
            if #img > 64 then
                out.image = img
                out.imageExt = npImageExt(img, mime)
            end
        end
    end
    return out
end

local function npVorbisComments(block, out)
    local vlen = npU32le(block, 1)
    local p = 5 + vlen
    local count = npU32le(block, p)
    p += 4
    for _ = 1, math.min(count, 200) do
        local len = npU32le(block, p)
        local kv = block:sub(p + 4, p + 3 + len)
        p = p + 4 + len
        local k, v = kv:match("^([^=]+)=(.*)$")
        if k then
            k = k:upper()
            if k == "TITLE" and not out.title then out.title = v end
            if k == "ARTIST" and not out.artist then out.artist = v end
        end
        if p > #block then break end
    end
end

local function npParseFLAC(data)
    if data:sub(1, 4) ~= "fLaC" then return nil end
    local out, pos = {}, 5
    for _ = 1, 64 do
        if pos + 3 > #data then break end
        local h = data:byte(pos)
        local last = h >= 128
        local btype = h % 128
        local len = (data:byte(pos + 1) * 256 + data:byte(pos + 2)) * 256 + data:byte(pos + 3)
        local block = data:sub(pos + 4, pos + 3 + len)
        pos = pos + 4 + len
        if btype == 4 then
            pcall(npVorbisComments, block, out)
        elseif btype == 6 and not out.image then
            local p = 5
            local mlen = npU32be(block, p)
            local mime = block:sub(p + 4, p + 3 + mlen)
            p = p + 4 + mlen
            local dlen = npU32be(block, p)
            p = p + 4 + dlen + 16
            local ilen = npU32be(block, p)
            local img = block:sub(p + 4, p + 3 + ilen)
            if #img > 64 then
                out.image = img
                out.imageExt = npImageExt(img, mime)
            end
        end
        if last then break end
    end
    return out
end

local function npParseOgg(data)
    if data:sub(1, 4) ~= "OggS" then return nil end
    local s = data:find("\3vorbis", 1, true)
    if not s then return nil end
    local out = {}
    pcall(npVorbisComments, data:sub(s + 7, s + 7 + 65536), out)
    return out
end

-- Returns { title, artist, cover, kind } (cover may be nil). Cached.
-- May yield (catalog lookup for ids), call it from a spawned thread.
function AnimMusic.meta(src)
    if type(src) ~= "string" or src == "" then return nil end
    local cached = AnimMusic.metaCache[src]
    if cached then return cached end

    local m = { title = nil, artist = nil, cover = nil }

    -- local file?
    local path
    local candidates = { AnimMusic.folder .. "/" .. src, src }
    if not src:match("%.[%w]+$") then
        for ext in pairs(AUDIO_EXT) do
            table.insert(candidates, AnimMusic.folder .. "/" .. src .. "." .. ext)
        end
    end
    for _, c in ipairs(candidates) do
        local ok, e = pcall(isfile, c)
        if ok and e then path = c break end
    end

    if path then
        m.kind = "file"
        local fname = path:match("([^/\\]+)$") or src
        m.title = fname:gsub("%.[%w]+$", "")
        local okR, data = pcall(readfile, path)
        if okR and type(data) == "string" then
            local tags
            local ok1, r1 = pcall(npParseID3, data)
            if ok1 and r1 then tags = r1 end
            if not tags then
                local ok2, r2 = pcall(npParseFLAC, data)
                if ok2 and r2 then tags = r2 end
            end
            if not tags then
                local ok3, r3 = pcall(npParseOgg, data)
                if ok3 and r3 then tags = r3 end
            end
            if tags then
                if tags.title and tags.title ~= "" then m.title = tags.title end
                if tags.artist and tags.artist ~= "" then m.artist = tags.artist end
                if tags.image and type(getcustomasset) == "function" then
                    pcall(function()
                        local dir = AnimMusic.folder .. "/covers"
                        if not isfolder(dir) then makefolder(dir) end
                        local safe = fname:gsub("[^%w_%-]", "_")
                        local cpath = dir .. "/" .. safe .. "." .. tags.imageExt
                        writefile(cpath, tags.image)
                        m.cover = getcustomasset(cpath)
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    else
        local id = src:match("^%s*(%d%d%d%d+)%s*$") or src:match("rbxassetid://(%d+)")
            or src:match("[?&]id=(%d+)") or src:match("/(%d%d%d%d%d+)")
        if id then
            m.kind = "id"
            m.title = "Audio " .. id
            m.cover = "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. id .. "&w=150&h=150"
            local ok, info = pcall(function()
                return game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(tonumber(id))
            end)
            if ok and type(info) == "table" then
                m.title = info.Name or m.title
                m.artist = info.Creator and info.Creator.Name or nil
            end
        else
            m.title = src
        end
    end

    AnimMusic.metaCache[src] = m
    return m
end


--==================================================
-- NOW PLAYING WINDOW
--==================================================
--
-- Its own draggable window. Shows the cover, title, artist, which
-- animation the track belongs to, time, and a SoundCloud-style waveform:
-- each bar is a slice of the song, filled in from the live loudness as
-- it plays (the whole track's shape is remembered after one listen),
-- played part lit, the rest dim. Click the waveform to seek.
-- Appears when music starts, hides when it stops.
--==================================================

AnimMusic.windowEnabled = true

local function buildNowPlaying()
    local pg = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    local old = pg:FindFirstChild("ReanimNowPlaying")
    if old then old:Destroy() end

    local BG      = Color3.fromRGB(12, 10, 24)
    local ACC     = Color3.fromRGB(160, 128, 255)
    local ACC2    = Color3.fromRGB(70, 120, 255)
    local DIMBAR  = Color3.fromRGB(86, 78, 136)
    local TXT     = Color3.fromRGB(238, 234, 252)
    local SUB     = Color3.fromRGB(184, 174, 224)
    local BARS    = 56
    local WF_H    = 30     -- bar area height (px)
    local REFL_H  = 8      -- reflection height (px)

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "ReanimNowPlaying"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    gui.DisplayOrder = 5
    gui.Parent = pg

    -- The CanvasGroup only handles the fade. Its own background stays
    -- empty: a UIGradient placed on a CanvasGroup tints EVERYTHING inside
    -- it (that's what made the whole window near-black), so the gradient
    -- lives on a separate background frame underneath the content.
    local win = Instance.new("CanvasGroup")
    win.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(350, 176)
    win.Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 1, -210)
    win.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    win.BorderSizePixel = 0
    win.GroupTransparency = 1
    win.Visible = false
    win.Parent = gui
    Instance.new("UICorner", win).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)

    local bgFill = Instance.new("Frame")
    bgFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    bgFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    bgFill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.04
    bgFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    bgFill.ZIndex = 0
    bgFill.Parent = win
    Instance.new("UICorner", bgFill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)
    do
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Rotation = 90
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(32, 24, 64), BG)
        g.Parent = bgFill
        local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        st.Color = ACC
        st.Transparency = 0.45
        st.Thickness = 1
        st.Parent = bgFill
    end

    -- cover with a glow that breathes with the music
    local glow = Instance.new("Frame")
    glow.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    glow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14 + 52, 14 + 52)
    glow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(104, 104)
    glow.BackgroundColor3 = ACC
    glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.8
    glow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    glow.Parent = win
    Instance.new("UICorner", glow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)

    local cover = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    cover.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14)
    cover.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(104, 104)
    cover.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 24, 60)
    cover.BorderSizePixel = 0
    cover.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    cover.Image = ""
    cover.Parent = win
    Instance.new("UICorner", cover).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

    local note = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    note.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    note.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    note.Text = "♪"
    note.TextColor3 = ACC
    note.TextSize = 46
    note.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    note.Parent = cover

    local function mkText(y, h, size, color, bold)
        local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        t.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(132, y)
        t.Size = UDim2.new(1, -164, 0, h)
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.TextColor3 = color
        t.TextSize = size
        t.Font = bold and Enum.Font.GothamBold or Enum.Font.Gotham
        t.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        t.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        t.Text = ""
        t.Parent = win
        return t
    end

    local tag    = mkText(16, 12, 9, ACC, true)
    tag.Text     = "NOW PLAYING"
    local title  = mkText(32, 22, 16, TXT, true)
    local artist = mkText(56, 16, 12, SUB, false)
    local onAnim = mkText(76, 14, 10, SUB, false)
    local timeL  = mkText(100, 14, 11, TXT, false)
    timeL.Size = UDim2.new(1, -146, 0, 14)

    local close = Instance.new("TextButton")
    close.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24)
    close.Position = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 8)
    close.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    close.Text = "×"
    close.TextColor3 = SUB
    close.TextSize = 20
    close.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    close.Parent = win

    -- waveform
    local wave = Instance.new("TextButton")
    wave.Text = ""
    wave.AutoButtonColor = false
    wave.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    wave.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 128)
    wave.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, WF_H + REFL_H + 2)
    wave.Parent = win

    local bars, refl = {}, {}
    for i = 1, BARS do
        local b = Instance.new("Frame")
        b.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
        b.Position = UDim2.new((i - 1) / BARS, 1, 0, WF_H)
        b.Size = UDim2.new(1 / BARS, -2, 0, 3)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = DIMBAR
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Parent = wave
        Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 1)
        bars[i] = b

        local r = Instance.new("Frame")
        r.Position = UDim2.new((i - 1) / BARS, 1, 0, WF_H + 2)
        r.Size = UDim2.new(1 / BARS, -2, 0, 1)
        r.BackgroundColor3 = DIMBAR
        r.BackgroundTransparency = 0.65
        r.BorderSizePixel = 0
        r.Parent = wave
        refl[i] = r
    end

    -- drag
    do
        local dragging, startPos, startMouse
        win.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                local rel = i.Position.Y - win.AbsolutePosition.Y
                if rel > 124 then return end   -- the waveform is for seeking
                dragging, startPos, startMouse = true, win.Position, i.Position
                local c
                c = i.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if i.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                        dragging = false
                        c:Disconnect()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local d = i.Position - startMouse
                win.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
            end
        end)
    end

    local dismissedKey = nil   -- × hides the window until the next track
    local currentKey   = nil
    local waveforms    = {}    -- [soundId] = { [bar] = 0..1 }
    local peak         = 1
    local level        = 0
    local shown        = false

    local function activeSound()
        local s = AnimMusic.preview
        if s and s.Parent then return s, AnimMusic.previewSrc, "preview" end
        s = AnimMusic.sound
        if s and s.Parent then
            return s, AnimMusic.soundSrc, AnimMusic.playing and AnimMusic.playing.anim
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function fmt(t)
        t = math.max(0, t or 0)
        return string.format("%d:%02d", math.floor(t / 60), math.floor(t % 60))
    end

    local function setShown(on)
        if on == shown then return end
        shown = on
        if on then win.Visible = true end
        local tw = TweenService:Create(win, TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad),
            { GroupTransparency = on and 0 or 1 })
        tw:Play()
        if not on then
            tw.Completed:Connect(function()
                if not shown then win.Visible = false end
            end)
        end
    end

    close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        dismissedKey = currentKey
        setShown(false)
    end)

    wave.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local snd = activeSound()
        if not (snd and snd.IsLoaded and snd.TimeLength > 0) then return end
        local mx = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation().X
        local rel = math.clamp((mx - wave.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(1, wave.AbsoluteSize.X), 0, 1)
        snd.TimePosition = rel * snd.TimeLength
    end)

    local function loadMeta(src, key)
        title.Text  = tostring(src or "")
        artist.Text = ""
        cover.Image = ""
        note.Visible = true
        task.spawn(function()
            local m = AnimMusic.meta(src)
            if currentKey ~= key or not m then return end
            title.Text  = m.title or tostring(src)
            artist.Text = m.artist or (m.kind == "id" and "Roblox audio" or "local file")
            if m.cover then
                cover.Image = m.cover
                note.Visible = false
            end
        end)
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        local snd, src, owner = activeSound()

        if not AnimMusic.windowEnabled or not snd then
            setShown(false)
            currentKey = nil
            return
        end

        local key = snd.SoundId .. "|" .. tostring(src)
        if key ~= currentKey then
            currentKey = key
            peak = 1
            loadMeta(src, key)
        end
        if dismissedKey == key then
            setShown(false)
            return
        end
        setShown(true)

        onAnim.Text = (owner == "preview") and "♪ preview"
            or ("♪ with  " .. tostring(owner))

        local len = snd.TimeLength
        local pos = snd.TimePosition
        timeL.Text = (snd.IsLoaded and len > 0) and (fmt(pos) .. "  /  " .. fmt(len)) or "loading …"

        -- live loudness, normalised against the track's own peak
        local loud = snd.PlaybackLoudness or 0
        peak = math.max(peak * (1 - dt * 0.05), loud, 1)
        local v = math.clamp(loud / peak, 0, 1)
        level = level + (v - level) * math.clamp(dt * 18, 0, 1)

        glow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(104 + level * 14, 104 + level * 14)
        glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.85 - level * 0.45

        local wf = waveforms[snd.SoundId]
        if not wf then
            wf = {}
            waveforms[snd.SoundId] = wf
        end

        local progress = (len > 0) and math.clamp(pos / len, 0, 1) or 0
        local cur = math.clamp(math.floor(progress * BARS) + 1, 1, BARS)
        if snd.IsPlaying and len > 0 then
            wf[cur] = math.max(wf[cur] or 0, v)
        end

        for i = 1, BARS do
            local h = wf[i]
            local played = (i < cur) or (i == cur and progress > 0)
            local px
            if h then
                px = math.max(3, math.floor(h * WF_H + 0.5))
            else
                px = 3
            end
            -- the bar being played right now pulses with the live level
            if i == cur then px = math.max(px, math.floor(level * WF_H + 0.5), 3) end

            local b, r = bars[i], refl[i]
            if b.Size.Y.Offset ~= px then
                b.Size = UDim2.new(1 / BARS, -2, 0, px)
                r.Size = UDim2.new(1 / BARS, -2, 0, math.max(1, math.floor(px * REFL_H / WF_H)))
            end
            local col = played and (i == cur and ACC2 or ACC) or DIMBAR
            if b.BackgroundColor3 ~= col then
                b.BackgroundColor3 = col
                r.BackgroundColor3 = col
            end
        end
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local ok, err = pcall(buildNowPlaying)
    if not ok then warn("[Music] now playing window failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
end)


end


--==================================================
-- SAVE / LOAD
--==================================================

local function saveAnim(name)
    local anim = Anims[name]
    if not anim then return false, "no such animation" end
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        if not isfolder(ANIM_FOLDER) then makefolder(ANIM_FOLDER) end
        writefile(ANIM_FOLDER .. "/" .. name .. ".json",
            HttpService:JSONEncode({
                name   = name,
                loop   = anim.loop,
                length = anim.length,
                key    = anim.key,
                linear = anim.linear,
                sourceId = anim.sourceId,
                music  = anim.music,
                musicVolume = anim.musicVolume,
                frames = anim.frames,
            }))
    end)
    return ok, err
end

local function loadAnimFile(name)
    local ok, data = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(ANIM_FOLDER .. "/" .. name .. ".json"))
    end)
    if not ok or not data or not data.frames then return false end

    -- Rebuild through copyPose so missing limbs get filled in
    local frames = {}
    for _, f in ipairs(data.frames) do
        table.insert(frames, { t = f.t, pose = copyPose(f.pose) })
    end

    Anims[data.name or name] = {
        loop   = data.loop and true or false,
        length = data.length or (frames[#frames] and frames[#frames].t) or 1,
        key    = (data.key and Enum.KeyCode[data.key]) and data.key or nil,
        linear = data.linear and true or nil,
        sourceId = data.sourceId,
        music  = data.music,
        musicVolume = data.musicVolume,
        frames = frames,
    }

    local exists = false
    for _, n in ipairs(ANIM_ORDER) do
        if n == (data.name or name) then exists = true break end
    end
    if not exists then table.insert(ANIM_ORDER, data.name or name) end

    return true
end

local function listAnimFiles()
    local out = {}
    pcall(function()
        if not isfolder(ANIM_FOLDER) then makefolder(ANIM_FOLDER) end
        for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(ANIM_FOLDER)) do
            local n = f:match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
            if n then table.insert(out, n) end
        end
    end)
    return out
end
