--==================================================
-- BODY TAB + HEADER BUTTON
--==================================================

function Ext.paintGrad(btn, c0, c1)
    local g = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
    if g then
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, c0),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, c1 or c0),
        })
    end
end

function Ext.buildHeaderButton(header, hLbl, H)
    hLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -132, 1, 0)

    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Size             = UDim2.new(0, 88, 0, 24)
    b.Position         = UDim2.new(1, -126, 0.5, -12)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = H.RED
    b.Text             = "DEANIMATE"
    b.TextColor3       = H.WHITE
    b.TextSize         = 10
    b.Font             = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.BorderSizePixel  = 0
    b.Parent           = header
    Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local function paint()
        local st = Ext.state()
        if st == "reanimated" then
            b.Text, b.BackgroundColor3 = "DEANIMATE", H.RED
        elseif st == "starting" then
            b.Text, b.BackgroundColor3 = "STARTING…", H.ORANGE
        elseif st == "deanimating" then
            b.Text, b.BackgroundColor3 = "RESPAWNING…", H.ORANGE
        else
            b.Text, b.BackgroundColor3 = "REANIMATE", H.GREEN
        end
    end

    b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local ok, err = Ext.toggle()
        if ok == false and err then warn("[Reanim] " .. tostring(err)) end
        paint()
    end)
    table.insert(Ext.listeners, paint)
    task.spawn(function()
        while b.Parent do
            paint()
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end

function Ext.buildBodyPage(page, H)
    local y = 10

    local function note(text, height)
        local l = H.label(page, text, y, H.DIM, 9)
        l.TextWrapped = true
        l.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, height)
        y += height + 4
        return l
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- reanimate / deanimate
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    H.label(page, "REANIMATE", y) y += 16
    local stateBtn = H.gradBtn(page, y, 32, "", H.BTN, H.BTN) y += 38
    local stateNote = note("", 28)

    stateBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local ok, err = Ext.toggle()
        if ok == false and err then warn("[Reanim] " .. tostring(err)) end
        Ext.changed()
    end)

    H.divider(page, y) y += 10

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- animations
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    H.label(page, "ANIMATIONS", y) y += 16
    local animBtn = H.gradBtn(page, y, 28, "", H.BTN, H.BTN) y += 34
    note("ROBLOX: Roblox's own idle / walk / run / jump / fall / climb / swim, plus /e dance, dance2, dance3, wave, point, laugh, cheer. Keybinds, imported animations and the editor still take over while they play.", 52)

    animBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Ext.setAnimMode(Ext.animMode == "roblox" and "custom" or "roblox")
    end)

    H.divider(page, y) y += 10

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- hide body parts
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    H.label(page, "HIDE BODY PARTS  (parked far below you, not deleted)", y) y += 16

    local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
    box.Size             = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 26)
    box.Position         = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, y)
    box.BackgroundColor3 = H.INPUT
    box.TextColor3       = H.WHITE
    box.PlaceholderText  = "head, left arm, legs…"
    box.PlaceholderColor3 = H.DIM
    box.Text             = ""
    box.TextSize         = 11
    box.Font             = Enum.Font.Gotham
    box.BorderSizePixel  = 0
    box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    box.Parent           = page
    Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

    local hideBtn = H.gradBtn(page, y, 26, "HIDE", H.RED, H.RED, 58, 166)
    local showBtn = H.gradBtn(page, y, 26, "SHOW", H.GREEN, H.GREEN, 58, 228)
    y += 32

    local feedback = H.label(page, "", y, H.DIM, 9) y += 16

    local function apply(on)
        local text = box.Text
        if text == "" then return end
        local parts, err = Ext.setHidden(text, on)
        if parts then
            feedback.Text = (on and "hidden: " or "shown: ") .. table.concat(parts, ", ")
            feedback.TextColor3 = H.DIM
            box.Text = ""
        else
            feedback.Text = err
            feedback.TextColor3 = H.ORANGE
        end
    end
    hideBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() apply(true) end)
    showBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() apply(false) end)
    box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter) if enter then apply(true) end end)

    local chips = {}
    local CHIP_NAMES = { "Head", "Torso", "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg" }
    for i, name in ipairs(CHIP_NAMES) do
        local col = (i - 1) % 3
        local row = math.floor((i - 1) / 3)
        local chip = H.gradBtn(page, y + row * 32, 26, name, H.BTN, H.BTN, 88, 10 + col * 94)
        chip.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            Ext.setHidden(name, not Ext.hidden[name])
        end)
        chips[name] = chip
    end
    y += 64

    local showAll = H.gradBtn(page, y, 26, "SHOW ALL", H.BTN, H.BTN) y += 32
    showAll.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Ext.setHidden("all", false) end)

    local hiddenNote = note("", 26)

    H.divider(page, y) y += 10

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- other players
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    H.label(page, "OTHER PLAYERS", y) y += 16
    local publicBtn = H.gradBtn(page, y, 28, "", H.BTN, H.BTN) y += 34
    publicBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        Ext.publicCommands = not Ext.publicCommands
        Ext.changed()
    end)
    note("When on, anyone can type -remove <part>, -unremove <part>, -play <id or name> and -stop to use them on your body (one command each per 1.5 s). Long ids can get hidden by Roblox's chat filter for them: dots get past it, like -play 507.771.019.", 64)

    H.divider(page, y) y += 10
    note("YOUR CHAT: -remove head · -unremove all · -play <id or name> · -stop · -deanimate · -reanimate · -anims roblox · -anims custom · /e dance (Roblox mode)", 52)

    page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y + 10)

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- live paint
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    local function paint()
        local st = Ext.state()
        if st == "reanimated" then
            stateBtn.Text = "DEANIMATE  ·  become a normal player"
            Ext.paintGrad(stateBtn, Color3.fromRGB(170, 50, 64), Color3.fromRGB(120, 34, 48))
            stateNote.Text = "Reanimated. DEANIMATE respawns you as a normal player right where you stand; REANIMATE brings this back."
        elseif st == "starting" then
            stateBtn.Text = "STARTING…  (press to cancel)"
            Ext.paintGrad(stateBtn, H.ORANGE, Color3.fromRGB(160, 100, 30))
            stateNote.Text = "Reanimate is booting. Pressing now cancels it and respawns you as a normal player."
        elseif st == "deanimating" then
            stateBtn.Text = "RESPAWNING…"
            Ext.paintGrad(stateBtn, H.ORANGE, Color3.fromRGB(160, 100, 30))
            stateNote.Text = "Waiting for the server to respawn you. You come back as a normal player."
        else
            stateBtn.Text = "REANIMATE"
            Ext.paintGrad(stateBtn, Color3.fromRGB(40, 150, 76), Color3.fromRGB(26, 100, 50))
            stateNote.Text = "Normal player. REANIMATE runs the whole reanimate again on this body."
        end

        local rbx = Ext.animMode == "roblox"
        animBtn.Text = rbx and "ANIMATIONS: ROBLOX DEFAULT" or "ANIMATIONS: YOUR OWN (keyframes)"
        if rbx then
            Ext.paintGrad(animBtn, Color3.fromRGB(40, 140, 70), Color3.fromRGB(26, 90, 46))
        else
            Ext.paintGrad(animBtn, H.BTN, H.BTN)
        end

        for name, chip in pairs(chips) do
            if Ext.hidden[name] then
                Ext.paintGrad(chip, Color3.fromRGB(170, 50, 64), Color3.fromRGB(120, 34, 48))
            else
                Ext.paintGrad(chip, H.BTN, H.BTN)
            end
        end

        publicBtn.Text = Ext.publicCommands and "OTHERS' COMMANDS: ON" or "OTHERS' COMMANDS: OFF"
        if Ext.publicCommands then
            Ext.paintGrad(publicBtn, Color3.fromRGB(40, 140, 70), Color3.fromRGB(26, 90, 46))
        else
            Ext.paintGrad(publicBtn, H.BTN, H.BTN)
        end

        local list = Ext.hiddenList()
        hiddenNote.Text = #list > 0 and ("hidden: " .. table.concat(list, ", ")) or "nothing hidden"
    end

    table.insert(Ext.listeners, paint)
    paint()
    task.spawn(function()
        while page.Parent do
            paint()
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end


--==================================================
-- 3D ANIMATOR  (ANIMATE tab → 3D EDIT)
--==================================================
-- Like Roblox's Animation Editor: click a body part to select its
-- track, drag the coloured rings to rotate it, the arrows to move it,
-- and fly the free camera around the pose. Everything writes straight
-- into the selected keyframe, exactly like the number boxes.
--
-- DriveFrame publishes, for every track, the frame the track's own
-- rotation is applied in ("pivot", at the joint) and the frame its
-- offset is applied in ("offset"). A ring drag rotates the pivot about
-- one of its own axes:   R' = R0 * fromAxisAngle(axis, angle)
-- and an arrow drag moves along one of its axes, converted into the
-- offset frame. Both are exact for every track type: R6 limbs, the
-- torso, R15 joints and props.
--==================================================

-- Distance along a ray to an oriented box, or nil
function Ext.rayBox(origin, dir, cf, size)
    local o = cf:PointToObjectSpace(origin)
    local d = cf:VectorToObjectSpace(dir)
    local h = size / 2
    local tmin, tmax = -math.huge, math.huge
    for _, ax in ipairs({ "X", "Y", "Z" }) do
        local oa, da, ha = o[ax], d[ax], h[ax]
        if math.abs(da) < 1e-9 then
            if oa < -ha or oa > ha then return nil end
        else
            local t1, t2 = (-ha - oa) / da, (ha - oa) / da
            if t1 > t2 then t1, t2 = t2, t1 end
            if t1 > tmin then tmin = t1 end
            if t2 < tmax then tmax = t2 end
            if tmin > tmax then return nil end
        end
    end
    if tmax < 0 then return nil end
    return tmin >= 0 and tmin or tmax
end

-- The part you see for a track (the rig part in rig mode)
function Ext.gizmoPartFor(name)
    local pr = findProp(name)
    if pr then return pr.handle end
    local r15 = string.match(name, "^R15:(.+)$")
    if r15 then return corpseParts[r15] end
    local rh = rigHandles[name]
    if rh and rh.Parent then return rh end
    if IS_R15 then
        if name == "Torso" then return corpseParts["LowerTorso"] or corpseParts["UpperTorso"] end
        if name == "Head" then return corpseParts["Head"] end
        for n, g in pairs(R15_DRIVER) do
            if g == name then return corpseParts[n] end
        end
        return nil
    end
    return corpseParts[name]
end

-- Which track sits under the mouse
function Ext.gizmoPick(screenPos)
    local cam = ws.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return nil end
    local ray = cam:ViewportPointToRay(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)
    local best, bestT = nil, math.huge
    for _, name in ipairs(POSE_PARTS) do
        local part = Ext.gizmoPartFor(name)
        if part and part.Parent then
            local t = Ext.rayBox(ray.Origin, ray.Direction, part.CFrame, part.Size)
            if t and t < bestT then
                best, bestT = name, t
            end
        end
    end
    return best
end

function Ext.gizmoRound(v, step)
    return math.floor(v / step + 0.5) * step
end

function Ext.gizmoPushUndo(pose, limb)
    local G = Ext.giz
    local l = pose[limb] or blankLimb()
    table.insert(G.undo, {
        pose = pose, limb = limb,
        vals = { rx = l.rx or 0, ry = l.ry or 0, rz = l.rz or 0,
                 ox = l.ox or 0, oy = l.oy or 0, oz = l.oz or 0 },
    })
    while #G.undo > 80 do table.remove(G.undo, 1) end
end

function Ext.gizmoUndo()
    local G = Ext.giz
    local u = table.remove(G.undo)
    if not u then return false end
    u.pose[u.limb] = u.pose[u.limb] or blankLimb()
    for k, v in pairs(u.vals) do u.pose[u.limb][k] = v end
    if G.hooks then G.hooks.changed() end
    return true
end

--==================================================
-- FREE CAMERA
--==================================================
-- WASD to fly, hold right mouse to look, E / Space up, Q down, Shift
-- faster, scroll wheel changes speed. Your character's movement keys
-- are sunk while it's on, so the body stays put while you fly.

function Ext.setFreecam(on)
    local G = Ext.giz
    on = on and true or false
    if G.cam == on then return end
    G.cam = on

    local cam = ws.CurrentCamera
    local CAS = game:GetService("ContextActionService")

    if on then
        G.prevCamType = cam.CameraType
        local cf = cam.CFrame
        local rx, ry = cf:ToOrientation()
        G.pitch, G.yaw, G.pos = rx, ry, cf.Position
        cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable

        pcall(function()
            CAS:BindActionAtPriority("DinoFreecamSink", function()
                return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
            end, false, Enum.ContextActionPriority.High.Value + 50,
                Enum.KeyCode.W, Enum.KeyCode.A, Enum.KeyCode.S, Enum.KeyCode.D,
                Enum.KeyCode.Q, Enum.KeyCode.E, Enum.KeyCode.Space,
                Enum.KeyCode.Up, Enum.KeyCode.Down, Enum.KeyCode.Left, Enum.KeyCode.Right)
        end)

        pcall(RunService.UnbindFromRenderStep, RunService, "DinoFreecam")
        RunService:BindToRenderStep("DinoFreecam", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1, function(dt)
            if not G.cam then return end
            local K = Enum.KeyCode
            local function down(k) return UserInputService:IsKeyDown(k) end

            if UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) then
                UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCurrentPosition
                local d = UserInputService:GetMouseDelta()
                G.yaw   = G.yaw - d.X * 0.0045
                G.pitch = math.clamp(G.pitch - d.Y * 0.0045, -1.55, 1.55)
            else
                UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
            end

            local move = Vector3.zero
            if down(K.W) or down(K.Up)    then move += Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end
            if down(K.S) or down(K.Down)  then move += Vector3.new(0, 0, 1) end
            if down(K.A) or down(K.Left)  then move += Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0) end
            if down(K.D) or down(K.Right) then move += Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end
            if down(K.E) or down(K.Space) then move += Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
            if down(K.Q) then move += Vector3.new(0, -1, 0) end

            local look = CFrame.fromOrientation(G.pitch, G.yaw, 0)
            local speed = G.speed * (down(K.LeftShift) and 3 or 1)
            if move.Magnitude > 0 then
                G.pos += look:VectorToWorldSpace(move.Unit) * speed * dt
            end
            ws.CurrentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(G.pos) * look
        end)
    else
        pcall(RunService.UnbindFromRenderStep, RunService, "DinoFreecam")
        pcall(function() CAS:UnbindAction("DinoFreecamSink") end)
        UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
        cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
        local c = driverChar or lp.Character
        local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then cam.CameraSubject = hum end
    end

    if Ext.gizmoPaint then pcall(Ext.gizmoPaint) end
end

--==================================================
-- 3D EDIT: panel rows + handles
--==================================================

function Ext.attachGizmo(hooks, page, y, H)
    local G = Ext.giz
    G.hooks = hooks

    H.label(page, "3D EDIT  (pick a keyframe, then click a body part)", y) y += 16

    local modeBtns = {}
    for i, def in ipairs({ { "ROTATE", "rotate" }, { "MOVE", "move" }, { "OFF", "off" } }) do
        local b = H.gradBtn(page, y, 24, def[1], H.BTN, H.BTN, 88, 10 + (i - 1) * 94)
        b.TextSize = 10
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            G.mode = def[2]
            Ext.gizmoPaint()
        end)
        modeBtns[def[2]] = b
    end
    y += 30

    local camBtn  = H.gradBtn(page, y, 24, "", H.BTN, H.BTN, 136, 10)
    local snapBtn = H.gradBtn(page, y, 24, "", H.BTN, H.BTN, 136, 152)
    camBtn.TextSize, snapBtn.TextSize = 10, 10
    camBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Ext.setFreecam(not G.cam) end)
    snapBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        G.snap = not G.snap
        Ext.gizmoPaint()
    end)
    y += 30

    local undoBtn = H.gradBtn(page, y, 24, "UNDO  (Ctrl+Z)", H.BTN, H.BTN, 136, 10)
    undoBtn.TextSize = 10
    undoBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() Ext.gizmoUndo() end)

    local selLbl = H.label(page, "", y + 5, H.DIM, 9)
    selLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 152, 0, y + 5)
    selLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 136, 0, 14)
    y += 30

    local help = H.label(page,
        "Rings rotate, arrows move, click a part to switch to it. Free cam: WASD, hold right mouse to look, E / Q up and down, Shift = fast, scroll = speed.",
        y, H.DIM, 9)
    help.TextWrapped = true
    help.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
    help.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 38)
    y += 42

    function Ext.gizmoPaint()
        for mode, b in pairs(modeBtns) do
            Ext.paintGrad(b, (G.mode == mode) and H.SEL or H.BTN)
        end
        camBtn.Text = G.cam and "FREE CAM: ON" or "FREE CAM: OFF"
        Ext.paintGrad(camBtn, G.cam and Color3.fromRGB(40, 140, 70) or H.BTN,
            G.cam and Color3.fromRGB(26, 90, 46) or H.BTN)
        snapBtn.Text = G.snap and "SNAP: 15° · 0.1" or "SNAP: OFF"
        Ext.paintGrad(snapBtn, G.snap and H.SEL or H.BTN)
        local _, limb = hooks.target()
        selLbl.Text = limb and ("selected: " .. shortName(limb)) or "no keyframe selected"
    end
    Ext.gizmoPaint()

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- 3D objects (local only: nothing here replicates)
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    local proxy = Instance.new("Part")
    proxy.Name         = "DinoGizmoPivot"
    proxy.Anchored     = true
    proxy.CanCollide   = false
    proxy.CanQuery     = false
    proxy.CanTouch     = false
    proxy.Transparency = 1
    proxy.Size         = Vector3.new(1.6, 1.6, 1.6)
    proxy.Parent       = ws.CurrentCamera

    local arcs = Instance.new("ArcHandles")
    arcs.Name   = "DinoRotate"
    arcs.Parent = hooks.gui

    local moves = Instance.new("Handles")
    moves.Name   = "DinoMove"
    moves.Style  = Enum.HandlesStyle.Movement
    moves.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 60)
    moves.Parent = hooks.gui

    local box = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
    box.Name                = "DinoSelected"
    box.Color3              = Color3.fromRGB(130, 95, 255)
    box.SurfaceColor3       = Color3.fromRGB(130, 95, 255)
    box.SurfaceTransparency = 0.88
    box.LineThickness       = 0.03
    box.Parent              = hooks.gui

    local function beginDrag()
        local pose, limb = hooks.target()
        if not pose then return nil end
        local fr = Ext.gizmoFrames[limb]
        if not fr then return nil end
        Ext.gizmoPushUndo(pose, limb)
        local l = pose[limb] or blankLimb()
        pose[limb] = l
        G.handleGrab = true
        G.drag = {
            pose = pose, limb = limb, l = l,
            start = { rx = l.rx or 0, ry = l.ry or 0, rz = l.rz or 0,
                      ox = l.ox or 0, oy = l.oy or 0, oz = l.oz or 0 },
            R0 = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(l.rx or 0), math.rad(l.ry or 0), math.rad(l.rz or 0)),
            pivot = fr.pivot, offset = fr.offset,
        }
        return G.drag
    end

    arcs.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() beginDrag() end)
    arcs.MouseDrag:Connect(function(axis, angle)
        local d = G.drag
        if not d then return end
        if G.snap then angle = math.rad(Ext.gizmoRound(math.deg(angle), 15)) end
        local R = d.R0 * CFrame.fromAxisAngle(Vector3.FromAxis(axis), angle)
        local x, yy, z = R:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
        d.l.rx = Ext.gizmoRound(math.deg(x), 0.1)
        d.l.ry = Ext.gizmoRound(math.deg(yy), 0.1)
        d.l.rz = Ext.gizmoRound(math.deg(z), 0.1)
        hooks.changed()
    end)
    arcs.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() G.drag = nil end)

    moves.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() beginDrag() end)
    moves.MouseDrag:Connect(function(face, distance)
        local d = G.drag
        if not d then return end
        if G.snap then distance = Ext.gizmoRound(distance, 0.1) end
        local world = d.pivot:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.FromNormalId(face)) * distance
        local delta = d.offset:VectorToObjectSpace(world)
        d.l.ox = Ext.gizmoRound(d.start.ox + delta.X, 0.001)
        d.l.oy = Ext.gizmoRound(d.start.oy + delta.Y, 0.001)
        d.l.oz = Ext.gizmoRound(d.start.oz + delta.Z, 0.001)
        hooks.changed()
    end)
    moves.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() G.drag = nil end)

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- click to select, Ctrl+Z, scroll = fly speed
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    local conns = {}
    table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
        if not Ext.alive() then return end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            G.pressAt = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Z and not gpe
            and (UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl)
                or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.RightControl)) then
            Ext.gizmoUndo()
        end
    end))

    table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, gpe)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
        G.drag = nil
        local pressAt = G.pressAt
        G.pressAt = nil
        if G.handleGrab then
            G.handleGrab = false
            return
        end
        if gpe or G.mode == "off" or not Ext.alive() then return end
        if not hooks.target() then return end
        local now = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        if not pressAt or (now - pressAt).Magnitude > 5 then return end
        local pick = Ext.gizmoPick(now)
        if pick then
            hooks.select(pick)
            Ext.gizmoPaint()
        end
    end))

    table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
        if G.cam and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
            G.speed = math.clamp(G.speed * (1.18 ^ input.Position.Z), 2, 150)
        end
    end))

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- every frame: follow the selected track
    ------------------------------------------------------------
    table.insert(conns, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not hooks.gui.Parent or not Ext.alive() then
            for _, c in ipairs(conns) do c:Disconnect() end
            pcall(proxy.Destroy, proxy)
            Ext.gizmoOn = false
            Ext.setFreecam(false)
            return
        end

        local pose, limb = hooks.target()
        Ext.gizmoOn = (G.mode ~= "off") and pose ~= nil

        local fr = Ext.gizmoOn and Ext.gizmoFrames[limb] or nil
        if fr then
            proxy.CFrame = fr.pivot
            arcs.Adornee  = (G.mode == "rotate") and proxy or nil
            moves.Adornee = (G.mode == "move") and proxy or nil
        else
            arcs.Adornee, moves.Adornee = nil, nil
        end

        box.Adornee = (pose and G.mode ~= "off") and Ext.gizmoPartFor(limb) or nil

        if limb ~= G.lastLimb or (pose ~= nil) ~= G.lastHadPose then
            G.lastLimb, G.lastHadPose = limb, (pose ~= nil)
            Ext.gizmoPaint()
        end
    end))

    return y
end


local function buildUI()
    local pg  = lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    local old = pg:FindFirstChild("AutorigReanimUI")
    if old then old:Destroy() end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name           = "AutorigReanimUI"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn   = false
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    gui.Parent         = pg

    local BG     = Color3.fromRGB(10, 9, 18)
    local PANEL  = Color3.fromRGB(17, 14, 32)
    local BTN    = Color3.fromRGB(26, 22, 50)
    local SEL    = Color3.fromRGB(74, 40, 188)
    local INPUT  = Color3.fromRGB(16, 14, 34)
    local ACCENT = Color3.fromRGB(92, 50, 225)
    local DIM    = Color3.fromRGB(130, 108, 215)
    local DIV    = Color3.fromRGB(36, 28, 70)
    local WHITE  = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    local GREEN  = Color3.fromRGB(45, 185, 95)
    local RED    = Color3.fromRGB(190, 55, 70)
    local ORANGE = Color3.fromRGB(225, 145, 40)

    local W = 300

    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name             = "Panel"
    frame.Size             = UDim2.new(0, W, 0, 540)
    frame.Position         = UDim2.new(1, -(W + 14), 0, 14)
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = BG
    frame.BorderSizePixel  = 0
    frame.ClipsDescendants = true
    frame.Parent           = gui
    Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)

    local fst = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    fst.Color = Color3.fromRGB(74, 42, 175)
    fst.Thickness = 1.5
    fst.Transparency = 0.45
    fst.Parent = frame

    -- Header ------------------------------------------------------
    local header = Instance.new("Frame")
    header.Size             = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38)
    header.BackgroundColor3 = PANEL
    header.BorderSizePixel  = 0
    header.Parent           = frame

    local hg = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    hg.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,   Color3.fromRGB(88, 46, 210)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(62, 42, 172)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,   Color3.fromRGB(40, 96, 230)),
    })
    hg.Parent = header

    local hLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    hLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -70, 1, 0)
    hLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
    hLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    hLbl.Text = "AUTORIG REANIMATE"
    hLbl.TextColor3 = WHITE
    hLbl.TextSize = 13
    hLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    hLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    hLbl.Parent = header

    local minBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    minBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
    minBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0.5, -13)
    minBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(32, 26, 68)
    minBtn.Text = "−"
    minBtn.TextColor3 = WHITE
    minBtn.TextSize = 18
    minBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    minBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    minBtn.Parent = header
    Instance.new("UICorner", minBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    Ext.buildHeaderButton(header, hLbl, { WHITE = WHITE, GREEN = GREEN, RED = RED, ORANGE = ORANGE })

    local dragging, dO, fO = false, nil, nil
    header.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            dragging, dO, fO = true, i.Position, frame.Position
        end
    end)
    header.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then dragging = false end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if dragging and i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
            local d = i.Position - dO
            frame.Position = UDim2.new(fO.X.Scale, fO.X.Offset + d.X,
                                       fO.Y.Scale, fO.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end
    end)

    -- Tab bar -----------------------------------------------------
    local tabBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    tabBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
    tabBar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 38)
    tabBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(13, 11, 25)
    tabBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    tabBar.Parent = frame

    local tSep = Instance.new("Frame")
    tSep.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
    tSep.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -1)
    tSep.BackgroundColor3 = DIV
    tSep.BorderSizePixel = 0
    tSep.Parent = tabBar

    local host = Instance.new("Frame")
    host.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -68)
    host.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 68)
    host.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    host.ClipsDescendants = true
    host.Parent = frame

    local function makePage(name)
        local sf = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        sf.Name = name
        sf.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        sf.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        sf.BorderSizePixel = 0
        sf.ScrollBarThickness = 3
        sf.ScrollBarImageColor3 = ACCENT
        sf.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        sf.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
        sf.Visible = false
        sf.Parent = host
        return sf
    end

    local pAnim   = makePage("anim")
    local pSword  = makePage("sword")
    local pFiles  = makePage("files")
    local pCam    = makePage("cam")
    local pRig    = makePage("rig")
    local pages   = { anim = pAnim, sword = pSword, files = pFiles, cam = pCam, rig = pRig }
    pages.body = makePage("body")

    local function showPage(n)
        for k, p in pairs(pages) do p.Visible = (k == n) end
    end

    local tabBtns = {}
    local TAB_DEFS = { {"ANIMATE","anim"}, {"BODY","body"}, {"RIG","rig"}, {"PROPS","sword"}, {"FILES","files"}, {"CAMERA","cam"} }
    for i, def in ipairs(TAB_DEFS) do
        local t = Instance.new("TextButton")
        t.Size = UDim2.new(1/#TAB_DEFS, 0, 1, 0)
        t.Position = UDim2.new((i-1)/#TAB_DEFS, 0, 0, 0)
        t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        t.Text = def[1]
        t.TextSize = 9
        t.TextColor3 = DIM
        t.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        t.BorderSizePixel = 0
        t.Parent = tabBar

        local ind = Instance.new("Frame")
        ind.Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 2)
        ind.Position = UDim2.new(0.2, 0, 1, -2)
        ind.BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT
        ind.BorderSizePixel = 0
        ind.Visible = false
        ind.Parent = t
        Instance.new("UICorner", ind).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

        local pn = def[2]
        t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            showPage(pn)
            for _, tb in ipairs(tabBtns) do
                tb.btn.TextColor3 = DIM
                tb.ind.Visible = false
            end
            t.TextColor3 = WHITE
            ind.Visible = true
        end)
        tabBtns[i] = { btn = t, ind = ind }
    end
    tabBtns[1].btn.TextColor3 = WHITE
    tabBtns[1].ind.Visible = true
    showPage("anim")

    local minimized, FULL_H = false, 540
    minBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        minimized = not minimized
        frame.Size = minimized and UDim2.new(0, W, 0, 38)
                                or UDim2.new(0, W, 0, FULL_H)
        minBtn.Text = minimized and "+" or "−"
    end)

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Shared widget builders
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    local function label(parent, txt, y, col, sz)
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        l.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 14)
        l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, y)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = txt
        l.TextColor3 = col or DIM
        l.TextSize = sz or 9
        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        l.Parent = parent
        return l
    end

    local function divider(parent, y)
        local d = Instance.new("Frame")
        d.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 1)
        d.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, y)
        d.BackgroundColor3 = DIV
        d.BorderSizePixel = 0
        d.Parent = parent
    end

    local function gradBtn(parent, y, h, txt, g0, g1, w, x)
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Size = w and UDim2.new(0, w, 0, h) or UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, h)
        b.Position = UDim2.new(0, x or 10, 0, y)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = g0
        b.Text = txt
        b.TextSize = 11
        b.TextColor3 = WHITE
        b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        b.BorderSizePixel = 0
        b.Parent = parent
        local gr = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        gr.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, g0),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, g1 or g0),
        })
        gr.Parent = b
        Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
        return b
    end

    -- Generic stepper row bound to a getter/setter
    local function stepRow(parent, y, name, step, fmt, get, set)
        local ll = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        ll.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 24)
        ll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, y)
        ll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ll.Text = name
        ll.TextColor3 = DIM
        ll.TextSize = 12
        ll.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        ll.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        ll.Parent = parent

        local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
        box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 24)
        box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 74, 0, y)
        box.BackgroundColor3 = INPUT
        box.TextColor3 = WHITE
        box.TextSize = 12
        box.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        box.BorderSizePixel = 0
        box.Text = string.format(fmt, get())
        box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        box.Parent = parent
        Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

        local bs = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        bs.Color = Color3.fromRGB(48, 38, 88)
        bs.Thickness = 1
        bs.Parent = box

        box.Focused:Connect(function() bs.Color = ACCENT bs.Thickness = 1.5 end)
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            bs.Color = Color3.fromRGB(48, 38, 88) bs.Thickness = 1
            local v = tonumber(box.Text)
            if v then set(v) end
            box.Text = string.format(fmt, get())
        end)

        local function mk(txt, sign, xOff)
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 24)
            b.Position = UDim2.new(0, xOff, 0, y)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 22, 58)
            b.Text = txt
            b.TextColor3 = DIM
            b.TextSize = 14
            b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Parent = parent
            Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local nv = math.floor((get() + sign * step) * 1000 + 0.5) / 1000
                set(nv)
                box.Text = string.format(fmt, get())
            end)
        end

        mk("−", -1, 182)
        mk("+",  1, 212)

        return function() box.Text = string.format(fmt, get()) end
    end

    Ext.buildBodyPage(pages.body, {
        label = label, divider = divider, gradBtn = gradBtn,
        BTN = BTN, INPUT = INPUT, ACCENT = ACCENT, DIM = DIM, WHITE = WHITE,
        GREEN = GREEN, RED = RED, ORANGE = ORANGE,
    })

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- ANIMATE PAGE
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    local editAnim  = "idle"
    local editFrame = nil     -- index into Anims[editAnim].frames
    local editLimb  = "Right Arm"

    local refreshAnimList, refreshFrameList, refreshLimbBtns, refreshAxes,
          refreshBind, refreshSlots
    local axisRefreshers = {}

    local function curAnim()  return Anims[editAnim] end
    local function curFrame()
        local a = curAnim()
        return a and editFrame and a.frames[editFrame] or nil
    end

    -- Push the selected frame's pose onto the rig
    local function applyEditPose()
        local f = curFrame()
        if f then
            editPose     = f.pose
            editorPaused = true
        else
            editPose     = nil
            editorPaused = false
        end
    end

    local ay = 10

    label(pAnim, "ANIMATION", ay) ay += 16

    -- Fixed-height scroller. AutomaticSize + UIGridLayout collapsed to
    -- zero height here and swallowed the whole section, so the buttons
    -- are positioned explicitly instead.
    local animListHolder = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    animListHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 58)
    animListHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, ay)
    animListHolder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(13, 11, 26)
    animListHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    animListHolder.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    animListHolder.ScrollBarImageColor3 = ACCENT
    animListHolder.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    animListHolder.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    animListHolder.Parent = pAnim
    Instance.new("UICorner", animListHolder).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)

    local alStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    alStroke.Color = DIV
    alStroke.Thickness = 1
    alStroke.Parent = animListHolder

    ay += 64

    local newAnimBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
    newAnimBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 128, 0, 24)
    newAnimBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, ay)
    newAnimBox.BackgroundColor3 = INPUT
    newAnimBox.TextColor3 = WHITE
    newAnimBox.PlaceholderText = "new animation name"
    newAnimBox.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 72, 120)
    newAnimBox.Text = ""
    newAnimBox.TextSize = 11
    newAnimBox.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    newAnimBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    newAnimBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    newAnimBox.Parent = pAnim
    Instance.new("UICorner", newAnimBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

    local newAnimBtn = gradBtn(pAnim, ay, 24, "+ NEW",
        Color3.fromRGB(40, 140, 70), Color3.fromRGB(26, 90, 46), 68, 144)

    -- Deep-copies the selected animation so you can branch a variant
    local copyAnimBtn = gradBtn(pAnim, ay, 24, "COPY",
        Color3.fromRGB(72, 38, 185), Color3.fromRGB(38, 96, 230), 68, 216)

    ay += 30

    local delAnimBtn = gradBtn(pAnim, ay, 22, "DELETE THIS ANIMATION",
        Color3.fromRGB(110, 34, 48), Color3.fromRGB(78, 22, 34))

    ay += 30

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Role slots — click to cycle which animation fills each role
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    label(pAnim, "ROLES", ay) ay += 16

    local slotBtns = {}
    for i, role in ipairs({ "idle", "walk", "swing" }) do
        local b = gradBtn(pAnim, ay, 24, "", BTN, BTN, 88, 10 + (i - 1) * 94)
        b.TextSize = 10
        slotBtns[role] = b

        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            -- advance to the next animation in the list
            local cur = Slots[role]
            local idx = 1
            for j, n in ipairs(ANIM_ORDER) do
                if n == cur then idx = j break end
            end
            Slots[role] = ANIM_ORDER[(idx % #ANIM_ORDER) + 1]
            currentRole = "idle"        -- force a clean re-evaluate
            refreshSlots()
        end)
    end

    refreshSlots = function()
        for role, b in pairs(slotBtns) do
            b.Text = string.upper(role) .. ": " .. tostring(Slots[role])
            local assigned = (Slots[role] == editAnim)
            local g = b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
            if g then
                g.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, assigned and SEL or BTN),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, assigned and SEL or BTN),
                })
            end
        end
    end

    ay += 32

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Keybind for the selected animation
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    label(pAnim, "KEYBIND", ay) ay += 16

    local bindBtn = gradBtn(pAnim, ay, 26, "None",
        Color3.fromRGB(46, 34, 92), Color3.fromRGB(34, 26, 70), 168, 10)

    local clearBindBtn = gradBtn(pAnim, ay, 26, "CLEAR",
        Color3.fromRGB(110, 34, 48), Color3.fromRGB(78, 22, 34), 102, 184)

    refreshBind = function()
        local a = curAnim()
        if keyListenFor then
            bindBtn.Text = "press a key…"
        elseif a and a.key then
            bindBtn.Text = "KEY:  " .. a.key
        else
            bindBtn.Text = "None  (click to bind)"
        end
    end

    bindBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        keyListenFor = editAnim
        refreshBind()
    end)

    clearBindBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local a = curAnim()
        if a then a.key = nil end
        keyListenFor = nil
        refreshBind()
    end)

    -- called from the global input handler once a key lands
    onKeyBound = function() refreshBind() end

    ay += 34
    divider(pAnim, ay) ay += 8

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Keyframe list
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    local frameHdr = label(pAnim, "KEYFRAMES", ay) ay += 16

    local frameList = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    frameList.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 104)
    frameList.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, ay)
    frameList.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(13, 11, 26)
    frameList.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frameList.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    frameList.ScrollBarImageColor3 = ACCENT
    frameList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    frameList.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    frameList.Parent = pAnim
    Instance.new("UICorner", frameList).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)

    local flStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    flStroke.Color = DIV
    flStroke.Thickness = 1
    flStroke.Parent = frameList

    local flLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    flLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
    flLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    flLayout.Parent = frameList

    local flPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    flPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)
    flPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4)
    flPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
    flPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4)
    flPad.Parent = frameList

    ay += 110

    -- Add / delete frame
    local timeBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
    timeBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 24)
    timeBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, ay)
    timeBox.BackgroundColor3 = INPUT
    timeBox.TextColor3 = WHITE
    timeBox.Text = "0.00"
    timeBox.TextSize = 11
    timeBox.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    timeBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    timeBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    timeBox.Parent = pAnim
    Instance.new("UICorner", timeBox).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

    local addFrameBtn = gradBtn(pAnim, ay, 24, "+ FRAME",
        Color3.fromRGB(72, 38, 185), Color3.fromRGB(38, 96, 230), 96, 72)

    local delFrameBtn = gradBtn(pAnim, ay, 24, "DELETE",
        Color3.fromRGB(150, 38, 55), Color3.fromRGB(100, 24, 38), 96, 176)

    ay += 32
    divider(pAnim, ay) ay += 8

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Limb selector
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    label(pAnim, "LIMB", ay) ay += 16

    local PROP_BTN = Color3.fromRGB(58, 40, 18)   -- props read as their own thing

    -- Scrolls, because the prop count is open-ended
    local limbHolder = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    limbHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 86)
    limbHolder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, ay)
    limbHolder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(13, 11, 26)
    limbHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    limbHolder.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    limbHolder.ScrollBarImageColor3 = ACCENT
    limbHolder.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    limbHolder.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    limbHolder.Parent = pAnim
    Instance.new("UICorner", limbHolder).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)

    local lhStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    lhStroke.Color = DIV lhStroke.Thickness = 1 lhStroke.Parent = limbHolder

    refreshLimbBtns = function()
        for _, ch in ipairs(limbHolder:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end
        end

        -- if the selected track vanished with a prop, fall back
        local stillThere = false
        for _, l in ipairs(POSE_PARTS) do
            if l == editLimb then stillThere = true break end
        end
        if not stillThere then editLimb = "Torso" end

        for i, l in ipairs(POSE_PARTS) do
            local col = (i - 1) % 3
            local row = math.floor((i - 1) / 3)
            local isProp = (findProp(l) ~= nil)

            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 84, 0, 24)
            b.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5 + col * 90, 0, 5 + row * 28)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = (l == editLimb) and SEL
                or (isProp and PROP_BTN or BTN)
            b.Text = shortName(l)
            b.TextSize = 10
            b.TextColor3 = WHITE
            b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Parent = limbHolder
            Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                editLimb = l
                refreshLimbBtns()
                refreshAxes()
            end)
        end

        local rows = math.ceil(#POSE_PARTS / 3)
        limbHolder.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, rows * 28 + 6)
    end

    refreshLimbBtns()

    ay += 92
    divider(pAnim, ay) ay += 8

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Axis editors
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    label(pAnim, "ROTATION  (degrees)", ay) ay += 16

    local function axisGetter(ax)
        return function()
            local f = curFrame()
            if not f then return 0 end
            local l = f.pose[editLimb]
            return l and l[ax] or 0
        end
    end

    local function axisSetter(ax)
        return function(v)
            local f = curFrame()
            if not f then return end
            if not f.pose[editLimb] then f.pose[editLimb] = blankLimb() end
            f.pose[editLimb][ax] = v
            applyEditPose()          -- push change straight to the rig
        end
    end

    for _, ax in ipairs({ "rx", "ry", "rz" }) do
        axisRefreshers[ax] = stepRow(pAnim, ay, string.upper(ax), 5, "%.1f",
            axisGetter(ax), axisSetter(ax))
        ay += 28
    end

    divider(pAnim, ay) ay += 8
    label(pAnim, "OFFSET  (studs)", ay) ay += 16

    for _, ax in ipairs({ "ox", "oy", "oz" }) do
        axisRefreshers[ax] = stepRow(pAnim, ay, string.upper(ax), 0.05, "%.2f",
            axisGetter(ax), axisSetter(ax))
        ay += 28
    end

    refreshAxes = function()
        for _, fn in pairs(axisRefreshers) do fn() end
    end

    ay += 4
    divider(pAnim, ay) ay += 8
    ay = Ext.attachGizmo({
        target  = function()
            local f = curFrame()
            if not f then return nil, nil end
            return f.pose, editLimb
        end,
        changed = function()
            applyEditPose()
            refreshAxes()
        end,
        select  = function(l)
            editLimb = l
            refreshLimbBtns()
            refreshAxes()
        end,
        gui     = gui,
    }, pAnim, ay, {
        label = label, divider = divider, gradBtn = gradBtn,
        BTN = BTN, SEL = SEL, DIM = DIM, WHITE = WHITE,
        GREEN = GREEN, RED = RED, ORANGE = ORANGE, ACCENT = ACCENT,
    })

    ay += 4
    divider(pAnim, ay) ay += 8

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Playback
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    label(pAnim, "PREVIEW", ay) ay += 16

    local playBtn = gradBtn(pAnim, ay, 28, "▶ PLAY",
        Color3.fromRGB(40, 140, 70), Color3.fromRGB(26, 90, 46), 88, 10)

    local stopBtn = gradBtn(pAnim, ay, 28, "■ LIVE",
        Color3.fromRGB(150, 38, 55), Color3.fromRGB(100, 24, 38), 88, 104)

    local loopBtn = gradBtn(pAnim, ay, 28, "↺ LOOP",
        BTN, BTN, 88, 198)

    ay += 34

    -- Separate from anim.loop: this only controls the editor preview,
    -- so you can loop a one-shot like the swing while building it.
    local pLoopBtn = gradBtn(pAnim, ay, 26,
        previewLoop and "⟳ PREVIEW LOOP: ON" or "⟳ PREVIEW LOOP: OFF",
        previewLoop and Color3.fromRGB(40, 140, 70) or BTN,
        previewLoop and Color3.fromRGB(26, 90, 46)  or BTN)

    pLoopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        previewLoop = not previewLoop
        pLoopBtn.Text = previewLoop and "⟳ PREVIEW LOOP: ON"
                                     or "⟳ PREVIEW LOOP: OFF"
        local g = pLoopBtn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
        if g then
            g.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, previewLoop
                    and Color3.fromRGB(40, 140, 70) or BTN),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, previewLoop
                    and Color3.fromRGB(26, 90, 46) or BTN),
            })
        end
    end)

    ay += 32

    local lengthRefresh = stepRow(pAnim, ay, "Length", 0.05, "%.2f",
        function() local a = curAnim() return a and a.length or 0 end,
        function(v)
            local a = curAnim()
            if a then a.length = math.max(0.05, v) end
        end)
    ay += 34

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Frame list rebuild
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    refreshFrameList = function()
        for _, ch in ipairs(frameList:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("Frame") or ch:IsA("TextLabel") then ch:Destroy() end
        end

        local a = curAnim()
        if not a or #a.frames == 0 then
            local e = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            e.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
            e.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            e.Text = "no keyframes"
            e.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 72, 120)
            e.TextSize = 10
            e.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            e.Parent = frameList
            frameList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 28)
            return
        end

        for i, f in ipairs(a.frames) do
            local row = Instance.new("Frame")
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
            row.BackgroundColor3 = (i == editFrame) and SEL or BTN
            row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            row.LayoutOrder = i
            row.Parent = frameList
            Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

            local tl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            tl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, 0)
            tl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
            tl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            tl.Text = string.format("Frame %d      t = %.2f s", i, f.t)
            tl.TextColor3 = WHITE
            tl.TextSize = 10
            tl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            tl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            tl.Parent = row

            local hit = Instance.new("TextButton")
            hit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            hit.Text = ""
            hit.Parent = row
            hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                editFrame = i
                timeBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", f.t)
                applyEditPose()
                refreshFrameList()
                refreshAxes()
            end)
        end

        frameList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #a.frames * 26 + 8)
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Animation list rebuild
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    refreshAnimList = function()
        for _, ch in ipairs(animListHolder:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end
        end

        local COLS, CW, CH2, GAP = 3, 84, 24, 6

        for i, n in ipairs(ANIM_ORDER) do
            local col = (i - 1) % COLS
            local row = math.floor((i - 1) / COLS)

            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(0, CW, 0, CH2)
            b.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5 + col * (CW + GAP), 0, 5 + row * (CH2 + GAP))
            b.BackgroundColor3 = (n == editAnim) and SEL or BTN
            b.Text = n
            b.TextSize = 10
            b.TextColor3 = WHITE
            b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.Parent = animListHolder
            Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                editAnim  = n
                editFrame = nil
                editPose  = nil
                editorPaused = false
                refreshAnimList()
                refreshFrameList()
                refreshAxes()
                lengthRefresh()
                refreshBind()
                refreshSlots()
                loopBtn.Text = (Anims[n] and Anims[n].loop)
                    and "↺ LOOP ON" or "↺ LOOP OFF"
            end)
        end

        local rows = math.ceil(#ANIM_ORDER / COLS)
        animListHolder.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, rows * (CH2 + GAP) + 6)
    end

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Button wiring
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    newAnimBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local n = newAnimBox.Text
        if n == "" or Anims[n] then
            newAnimBtn.Text = "BAD NAME"
            task.delay(1.2, function() newAnimBtn.Text = "+ NEW" end)
            return
        end
        Anims[n] = {
            loop   = true,
            length = 1.0,
            frames = { { t = 0.0, pose = blankPose() } },
        }
        table.insert(ANIM_ORDER, n)
        editAnim  = n
        editFrame = 1
        newAnimBox.Text = ""
        refreshAnimList()
        refreshFrameList()
        applyEditPose()
        refreshAxes()
        lengthRefresh()
        refreshBind()
        refreshSlots()
    end)

    local function selectAnim(n)
        editAnim  = n
        editFrame = nil
        editPose  = nil
        editorPaused = false
        refreshAnimList()
        refreshFrameList()
        refreshAxes()
        lengthRefresh()
        refreshBind()
        refreshSlots()
        loopBtn.Text = (Anims[n] and Anims[n].loop)
            and "↺ LOOP ON" or "↺ LOOP OFF"
    end

    copyAnimBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local srcAnim = curAnim()
        if not srcAnim then return end

        -- use the typed name, else auto-number a copy
        local n = newAnimBox.Text
        if n == "" then
            local base, i = editAnim .. " copy", 1
            n = base
            while Anims[n] do i = i + 1 n = base .. " " .. i end
        end

        if Anims[n] then
            copyAnimBtn.Text = "EXISTS"
            task.delay(1.2, function() copyAnimBtn.Text = "COPY" end)
            return
        end

        Anims[n] = cloneAnim(srcAnim)
        table.insert(ANIM_ORDER, n)
        newAnimBox.Text = ""
        selectAnim(n)

        copyAnimBtn.Text = "COPIED"
        task.delay(1.2, function() copyAnimBtn.Text = "COPY" end)
    end)

    delAnimBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if #ANIM_ORDER <= 1 then
            delAnimBtn.Text = "NEED AT LEAST ONE"
            task.delay(1.4, function()
                delAnimBtn.Text = "DELETE THIS ANIMATION" end)
            return
        end

        local gone = editAnim
        Anims[gone] = nil
        for i, n in ipairs(ANIM_ORDER) do
            if n == gone then table.remove(ANIM_ORDER, i) break end
        end

        -- any role pointing at it falls back to the first animation
        for role, n in pairs(Slots) do
            if n == gone then Slots[role] = ANIM_ORDER[1] end
        end
        if manualAnim == gone then manualAnim = nil end

        selectAnim(ANIM_ORDER[1])
    end)

    addFrameBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local a = curAnim()
        if not a then return end

        local t = tonumber(timeBox.Text) or 0
        -- Copy whatever is currently on the rig so you can pose then commit
        local src = curFrame() and curFrame().pose or playState.livePose
        local nf  = { t = t, pose = copyPose(src) }

        -- Replace a frame at the same time, otherwise insert in order
        local placed = false
        for i, f in ipairs(a.frames) do
            if math.abs(f.t - t) < 0.001 then
                a.frames[i] = nf editFrame = i placed = true break
            elseif f.t > t then
                table.insert(a.frames, i, nf) editFrame = i placed = true break
            end
        end
        if not placed then
            table.insert(a.frames, nf)
            editFrame = #a.frames
        end

        if t > a.length then a.length = t end

        timeBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", t + 0.25)
        applyEditPose()
        refreshFrameList()
        refreshAxes()
        lengthRefresh()
    end)

    delFrameBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local a = curAnim()
        if not a or not editFrame then return end
        table.remove(a.frames, editFrame)
        editFrame = nil
        editPose = nil
        editorPaused = false
        refreshFrameList()
        refreshAxes()
    end)

    playBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        editFrame    = nil
        editPose     = nil
        editorPaused = true          -- hold the state machine off
        previewName  = editAnim
        playAnim(editAnim, true)
        refreshFrameList()
    end)

    stopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        editFrame    = nil
        editPose     = nil
        previewName  = nil
        editorPaused = false         -- hand control back to the state machine
        refreshFrameList()
    end)

    loopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local a = curAnim()
        if not a then return end
        a.loop = not a.loop
        loopBtn.Text = a.loop and "↺ LOOP ON" or "↺ LOOP OFF"
    end)

    pAnim.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, ay + 10)

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Attach point + grip for the selected prop
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    label(pSword, "ATTACH TO", sy) sy += 16

    local attachBtn = gradBtn(pSword, sy, 26, "Right Arm",
        Color3.fromRGB(46, 34, 92), Color3.fromRGB(34, 26, 70))
    attachBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local pr = selProp and findProp(selProp)
        if not pr then return end
        local idx = 1
        for i, l in ipairs(LIMBS) do
            if l == pr.attach then idx = i break end
        end
        pr.attach = LIMBS[(idx % #LIMBS) + 1]
        -- reset to that limb's sane default so it doesn't fly off
        local g = DEFAULT_GRIP[pr.attach]
        if g then
            pr.gx, pr.gy, pr.gz    = g.gx, g.gy, g.gz
            pr.grx, pr.gry, pr.grz = g.grx, g.gry, g.grz
        end
        refreshPropCfg()
    end)
    sy += 34

    label(pSword, "GRIP POSITION", sy) sy += 16

    local gripRefreshers = {}

    local function propGet(field)
        return function()
            local pr = selProp and findProp(selProp)
            return pr and pr[field] or 0
        end
    end
    local function propSet(field)
        return function(v)
            local pr = selProp and findProp(selProp)
            if pr then pr[field] = v end
        end
    end

    for _, def in ipairs({ {"X","gx"}, {"Y","gy"}, {"Z","gz"} }) do
        gripRefreshers[def[2]] = stepRow(pSword, sy, def[1], 0.05, "%.2f",
            propGet(def[2]), propSet(def[2]))
        sy += 28
    end

    divider(pSword, sy) sy += 8
    label(pSword, "GRIP ROTATION  (degrees)", sy) sy += 16

    for _, def in ipairs({ {"RX","grx"}, {"RY","gry"}, {"RZ","grz"} }) do
        gripRefreshers[def[2]] = stepRow(pSword, sy, def[1], 5, "%.0f",
            propGet(def[2]), propSet(def[2]))
        sy += 28
    end

    slPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4) slPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4)
    slPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4) slPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4)
    slPad.Parent = scanList

        function() return CONFIG.swingSpeed end,
        function(v) CONFIG.swingSpeed = math.max(0.05, v) end)

        editorPaused = false
        editPose = nil
        swingQueued = true

    local regrabBtn = gradBtn(pSword, sy, 28, "RE-SEND -GH",

    regrabBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        send("-gh " .. GH_IDS)
        regrabBtn.Text = "SENT"
        task.delay(1.5, function() regrabBtn.Text = "RE-SEND -GH" end)
    end)

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- Prop list / config / scan refreshers
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    refreshPropCfg = function()
        local pr = selProp and findProp(selProp)
        attachBtn.Text = pr and pr.attach or "—"
        for _, fn in pairs(gripRefreshers) do fn() end
    end

    refreshProps = function()
        for _, ch in ipairs(propList:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("TextButton") or ch:IsA("TextLabel") then ch:Destroy() end
        end

        if #Props == 0 then
            local e = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            e.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
            e.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            e.Text = "no props — scan below"
            e.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 72, 120)
            e.TextSize = 10
            e.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            e.Parent = propList
            propList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 26)
            selProp = nil
            refreshPropCfg()
            return
        end

        if not (selProp and findProp(selProp)) then
            selProp = Props[1].id
        end

        for i, pr in ipairs(Props) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = (pr.id == selProp) and SEL or BTN
            b.Text = "  " .. pr.id .. "   →  " .. pr.attach
            b.TextSize = 10
            b.TextColor3 = WHITE
            b.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.LayoutOrder = i
            b.Parent = propList
            Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                selProp = pr.id
                refreshProps()
                refreshPropCfg()
            end)
        end

        propList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #Props * 27 + 8)
        refreshPropCfg()
    end

    refreshScan = function()
        for _, ch in ipairs(scanList:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("TextButton") or ch:IsA("TextLabel") then ch:Destroy() end
        end

        local c = driverChar or lp.Character
        local found = {}

        if c then
            for _, v in ipairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("Accessory") then
                    -- skip ones already registered as props
                    local taken = false
                    for _, pr in ipairs(Props) do
                        if pr.handle and pr.handle:IsDescendantOf(v) then
                            taken = true break
                        end
                    end
                    -- skip the invisible driver's duplicates
                    local h = v:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                    if h and not taken and h.Transparency < 1 then
                        table.insert(found, { acc = v, handle = h })
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if #found == 0 then
            local e = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            e.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
            e.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            e.Text = "nothing unregistered found"
            e.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 72, 120)
            e.TextSize = 10
            e.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            e.Parent = scanList
            scanList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 26)
            return
        end

        for i, entry in ipairs(found) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = BTN
            b.Text = "  + " .. entry.acc.Name
            b.TextSize = 10
            b.TextColor3 = WHITE
            b.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.LayoutOrder = i
            b.Parent = scanList
            Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)

            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local pr = addProp(entry.acc, entry.handle,
                    driverChar or lp.Character, "Right Arm")
                if pr then selProp = pr.id end
                refreshScan()
            end)
        end

        scanList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #found * 27 + 8)
    end

    scanBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(refreshScan)

    removeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if not selProp then return end
        removeProp(selProp)
        selProp = nil
        refreshScan()
    end)

    -- addProp/removeProp call this so every view stays in sync
    onPropsChanged = function()
        refreshProps()
        refreshLimbBtns()
        refreshAxes()
    end

    refreshProps()
    refreshScan()

    ----------------------------------------------------------------
    -- FILES PAGE
    ----------------------------------------------------------------

    local fy = 10
    local refreshFilesRef = nil

    label(pFiles, "PLAY ANIMATION BY ID  (marketplace · emotes · in-game)", fy) fy += 16

    local function mkBox(y, placeholder)
        local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
        box.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 26)
        box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, y)
        box.BackgroundColor3 = INPUT
        box.TextColor3 = WHITE
        box.PlaceholderText = placeholder
        box.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 82, 130)
        box.Text = ""
        box.TextSize = 12
        box.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        box.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        box.BorderSizePixel = 0
        box.Parent = pFiles
        Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
        pad.Parent = box
        local st = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(48, 38, 88)
        st.Thickness = 1
        st.Parent = box
        box.Focused:Connect(function() st.Color = ACCENT end)
        box.FocusLost:Connect(function() st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(48, 38, 88) end)
        return box
    end

    local idBox   = mkBox(fy, "animation id or link") fy += 32
    local nameBox = mkBox(fy, "name (optional, default anim_<id>)") fy += 32

    -- nil = use whatever the animation itself says
    local importLoop = nil

    end

        if importLoop == nil then importLoop = true
        elseif importLoop then importLoop = false
        else importLoop = nil end
        paintLoopMode()
    end)

        paintR15()

    local musicBox = mkBox(fy, "selected file, or a Roblox audio id") fy += 32

    local selectedMusic = nil
    local musicRows = {}

    local function paintMusicRows()
        for name, b in pairs(musicRows) do
            local on = (name == selectedMusic)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(72, 38, 185) or BTN
        end
    end

    local function refreshMusicList()
        for _, ch in ipairs(musicList:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("TextButton") or ch:IsA("TextLabel") then ch:Destroy() end
        end
        musicRows = {}
        local names = AnimMusic.list()
        if #names == 0 then
            local e = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            e.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
            e.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            e.Text = "no audio files yet\nput mp3/ogg in workspace/" .. AnimMusic.folder .. " → ↻"
            e.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 72, 120)
            e.TextSize = 10
            e.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            e.TextWrapped = true
            e.Parent = musicList
            musicList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 44)
            return
        end
        for i, n in ipairs(names) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
            b.BackgroundColor3 = BTN
            b.Text = "  ♪  " .. n
            b.TextSize = 11
            b.TextColor3 = WHITE
            b.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            b.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.LayoutOrder = i
            b.Parent = musicList
            Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                selectedMusic = n
                musicBox.Text = n
                paintMusicRows()
            end)
            musicRows[n] = b
        end
        musicList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, #names * 25 + 8)
        paintMusicRows()
    end

    musicBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        if musicRows[musicBox.Text] then
            selectedMusic = musicBox.Text
        else
            selectedMusic = nil
        end
        paintMusicRows()
    end)

    local thirdW = math.floor((W - 32) / 3)

    -- With this on, whatever track is selected is put on every animation
    -- you LOAD + PLAY by id, so it starts together with it. SET is for

    local attachToImports = true

            or  "SELECTED TRACK GOES ON NEXT LOAD + PLAY: OFF"

        end,
        function(v)
            local a = Anims[editAnim]
            if a then a.musicVolume = math.clamp(v, 0, 10) end
            if AnimMusic.preview then AnimMusic.preview.Volume = math.clamp(v, 0, 10) end
        end)

    local function paintMusic()
        local a = Anims[editAnim]
        musicHead.Text = "SET TARGETS  " .. string.upper(tostring(editAnim))
            .. ((a and a.music and a.music ~= "") and ("  ·  ♪ " .. a.music) or "  ·  none")
        musicStatus.Text = AnimMusic.status
        musicStatus.TextColor3 = (AnimMusic.statusOk == true and GREEN)
            or (AnimMusic.statusOk == false and RED) or DIM
        musicNote.Text = (AnimMusic.othersHear()
            and "This game lets client sounds replicate: other players hear it too. "
            or "Only you hear it in this game (client sounds don't replicate here). ")
            .. "Spotify can't play in Roblox (DRM). The music starts when the animation plays."
    end
    AnimMusic.onStatus = paintMusic

    musicRefresh.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        refreshMusicList()
        musicRefresh.Text = "✓"
        task.delay(0.8, function() musicRefresh.Text = "↻ REFRESH" end)
    end)

    musicPreview.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local a = Anims[editAnim]
        local on = AnimMusic.previewToggle(musicBox.Text, a and a.musicVolume or 1)
        musicPreview.Text = on and "■ STOP" or "▶ PREVIEW"
        paintMusic()
    end)

    musicSet.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local a = Anims[editAnim]
        if not a then return end
        local src = (musicBox.Text or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        local resolved, why = AnimMusic.resolve(src)
        if not resolved then
            musicSetStatus("✕ " .. tostring(why), false)
            musicSet.Text = "✕"
            task.delay(1.2, function() musicSet.Text = "SET" end)
            return
        end
        a.music = src
        a.musicVolume = a.musicVolume or 1
        local slotNote = ""
        for role, slotAnim in pairs(Slots) do
            if slotAnim == editAnim then
                slotNote = " · NOTE: " .. editAnim .. " is your " .. role
                    .. " animation, so it plays every time you " .. role
                break
            end
        end
        musicSetStatus("♪ " .. src .. " set on " .. tostring(editAnim) .. slotNote .. " · SAVE to keep", true)
        musicSet.Text = "✓"
        task.delay(1.2, function() musicSet.Text = "SET" end)
        paintMusic()
    end)

    musicClr.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local a = Anims[editAnim]
        if a then a.music = nil end
        paintMusic()
    end)

    refreshMusicList()
    task.spawn(function()
        while pFiles and pFiles.Parent do
            paintMusic()
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)

    AnimImport.onImported = function(name)
        lastImported = name

        -- put the selected track on the new animation so it starts with it
        local track = (musicBox.Text or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
        local a = Anims[name]
        if attachToImports and a and track ~= "" and AnimMusic.resolve(track) then
            a.music = track
            a.musicVolume = a.musicVolume or 1
        end
        editAnim  = name
        editFrame = nil
        pcall(refreshAnimList)
        pcall(refreshFrameList)
        pcall(refreshAxes)
        pcall(lengthRefresh)
        pcall(refreshBind)
        pcall(refreshSlots)
    end

    playIdBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        importStatus.Text = "loading …"
        importStatus.TextColor3 = DIM
        playIdBtn.Text = "LOADING …"
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, nameOrErr, info = AnimImport.loadAndPlay(idBox.Text, nameBox.Text, { loop = importLoop })
            playIdBtn.Text = "LOAD + PLAY"
            if ok then
                importStatus.Text = "▶ " .. nameOrErr .. " · " .. info
                importStatus.TextColor3 = GREEN
            else
                importStatus.Text = "✕ " .. tostring(nameOrErr)
                importStatus.TextColor3 = RED
            end
        end)
    end)

    stopIdBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        manualAnim   = nil
        editPose     = nil
        editorPaused = false
        importStatus.Text = "stopped · back to idle/walk"
        importStatus.TextColor3 = DIM
    end)

    saveImpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local n = lastImported or editAnim
        local ok = saveAnim(n)
        saveImpBtn.Text = ok and ("SAVED  " .. n) or "SAVE FAILED"
        task.delay(1.6, function() saveImpBtn.Text = "SAVE LAST IMPORTED" end)
        if ok and refreshFilesRef then pcall(refreshFilesRef) end
    end)

    divider(pFiles, fy) fy += 8
    label(pFiles, "SAVE CURRENT ANIMATION", fy) fy += 16

    local saveBtn = gradBtn(pFiles, fy, 28, "SAVE",
        Color3.fromRGB(40, 140, 70), Color3.fromRGB(26, 90, 46))
    saveBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local ok = saveAnim(editAnim)
        saveBtn.Text = ok and ("SAVED  " .. editAnim) or "SAVE FAILED"
        task.delay(1.6, function() saveBtn.Text = "SAVE" end)
    end)

    local exportBtn = gradBtn(pFiles, fy, 28, "COPY AS LUA",
        Color3.fromRGB(72, 38, 185), Color3.fromRGB(38, 96, 230))
    exportBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local a = curAnim()
        if not a then return end
        local parts = {}
        for _, f in ipairs(a.frames) do
            local limbs = {}
            for _, l in ipairs(POSE_PARTS) do
                local p = f.pose[l]
                local bits = {}
                for _, ax in ipairs(AXES) do
                    if math.abs(p[ax] or 0) > 0.001 then
                        table.insert(bits, string.format("%s = %g", ax, p[ax]))
                    end
                end
                if #bits > 0 then
                    table.insert(limbs, string.format(
                        '            ["%s"] = { %s },', l, table.concat(bits, ", ")))
                end
            end
            table.insert(parts, string.format(
                "        { t = %.2f, pose = pose({\n%s\n        })},",
                f.t, table.concat(limbs, "\n")))
        end
        pcall(setclipboard, string.format(
            'Anims["%s"] = {\n    loop   = %s,\n    length = %.2f,\n    key    = %s,\n    frames = {\n%s\n    },\n}',
            editAnim, tostring(a.loop), a.length,
            a.key and ('"' .. a.key .. '"') or "nil",
            table.concat(parts, "\n")))
        exportBtn.Text = "COPIED ✓"
        task.delay(1.4, function() exportBtn.Text = "COPY AS LUA" end)
    end)
    fy += 36

    divider(pFiles, fy) fy += 8
    label(pFiles, "SAVED FILES", fy) fy += 16

    local fileList = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    fileList.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 130)
    fileList.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, fy)
    fileList.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(13, 11, 26)
    fileList.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fileList.ScrollBarThickness = 3
