    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- toggles
    ------------------------------------------------------------

    local toggleDefs = {
        { "TOOL FIREWALL",  "ToolFirewall", nil },
        { "PICKUP SHIELD",  "PickupShield", function(on)
            if not on and TouchOff then SetBodyTouch(true) end
        end },
        { "KNOCKDOWN LOCK", "StateLock",    function() ApplyStateLock() end },
        { "VOID SHIELD",    "VoidShield",   function() ApplyVoidShield() end },
        { "ANCHOR LOCK",    "AnchorLock",   function(on)
            if not on then ForceReleaseAnchor() end
        end },
        { "TOASTS",         "Notifications", nil },
    }

    local rows = math.ceil(#toggleDefs / 2)
    local toggles = Section(rows * 26 + (rows - 1) * 6)
    Make("UIGridLayout", {
        CellSize    = UDim2.new(0.5, -4, 0, 26),
        CellPadding = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 6),
        SortOrder   = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        Parent      = toggles,
    })

    for i, def in ipairs(toggleDefs) do

        local name, key, callback = def[1], def[2], def[3]

        local button = Make("TextButton", {
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel,
            AutoButtonColor  = false,
            Text             = "",
            LayoutOrder      = i,
            Parent           = toggles,
        }, { Corner(6) })
        local buttonStroke = Stroke(Theme.Green, 1, 0.75)
        buttonStroke.Parent = button
        Hover(button, Theme.Panel, Theme.Hover)

        local dot = Make("Frame", {
            AnchorPoint      = Vector2.new(0, 0.5),
            Position         = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0.5, 0),
            Size             = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10),
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Green,
            BorderSizePixel  = 0,
            Parent           = button,
        }, { Corner(3) })
        Stroke(Theme.Green, 1, 0.3).Parent = dot

        local caption = Label({
            Text     = name,
            TextSize = 11,
            Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 0),
            Size     = UDim2.new(1, -30, 1, 0),
            Parent   = button,
        })

        local function Paint()
            local on = CFG[key] == true
            TweenService:Create(dot, TweenInfo.new(0.15),
                { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1 }):Play()
            caption.TextColor3 = on and Theme.Text or Theme.Dim
            buttonStroke.Transparency = on and 0.55 or 0.85
        end
        Paint()

        button.Activated:Connect(function()
            CFG[key] = not CFG[key]
            if callback then pcall(callback, CFG[key]) end
            Paint()
            LogEvent("UI", name .. (CFG[key] and " on" or " off"))
        end)
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------

    local inv = Section(26)
    local hiddenText = Label({
        Text       = "HIDDEN TOOLS: 0",
        TextSize   = 11,
        TextColor3 = Theme.Dim,
        Size       = UDim2.new(1, -152, 1, 0),
        Parent     = inv,
    })

    local function SmallButton(text, offsetX, onClick)
        local b = Make("TextButton", {
            AnchorPoint      = Vector2.new(1, 0),
            Position         = UDim2.new(1, offsetX, 0, 0),
            Size             = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 26),
            BackgroundColor3 = Theme.Panel2,
            AutoButtonColor  = false,
            Text             = text,
            Font             = UIFont,
            TextSize         = 11,
            TextColor3       = Theme.Green,
            Parent           = inv,
        }, { Corner(6) })
        Stroke(Theme.Green, 1, 0.5).Parent = b
        Hover(b, Theme.Panel2, Theme.Hover)
        b.Activated:Connect(onClick)
        return b
    end

    SmallButton("RESTORE", 0, function()
        local n = RestoreHiddenTools()
        UI.Toast("TOOLS", n .. " hidden tool(s) put back in your inventory")
    end)
    SmallButton("CLEAN", -78, function()
        local n = CleanInventory(true)
        UI.Toast("TOOLS", n .. " picked-up tool(s) taken out of your inventory")
    end)

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- live log
    ------------------------------------------------------------

        Text       = "LIVE LOG",

    local feedCountText = Label({
        Text           = "0 events",
        TextSize       = 11,
        TextColor3     = Theme.Dim,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
        Position       = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0),
        Size           = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0),
        Parent         = feedHead,

    local feedBox = Section(112)
    local feed = Make("ScrollingFrame", {
        Size                 = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
        BackgroundColor3     = Theme.Panel,
        BorderSizePixel      = 0,
        ScrollBarThickness   = 3,
        ScrollBarImageColor3 = Theme.Green,
        CanvasSize           = UDim2.new(),
        AutomaticCanvasSize  = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        ScrollingDirection   = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
        Parent               = feedBox,
    }, {
        Corner(6),
        Make("UIPadding", {
            PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6),
            PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8),
        }),
        Make("UIListLayout", {
            Padding   = UDim.new(0, 3),
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        }),
    })
    Stroke(Theme.Green, 1, 0.8).Parent = feed

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- footer
    ------------------------------------------------------------

    local footer = Section(16)
    local footLeft = Label({
        Text       = "Gui Ready",
        TextColor3 = Theme.Green,
        Size       = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 1, 0),
        Parent     = footer,
    })
    Label({
        Text           = "Toggle with " .. CFG.UIToggleKey.Name,
        TextSize       = 11,
        TextColor3     = Theme.Dim,
        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
        Position       = UDim2.fromScale(0.45, 0),
        Size           = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 1, 0),
        Parent         = footer,

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- size, fit to screen, minimise, drag, hotkey
    ------------------------------------------------------------

    local FULL = fullHeight
    local MINI = PAD * 2 + HEADER
    local minimized = false

    main.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(WIDTH, FULL)

    local function Fit()
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        local s = math.clamp((cam.ViewportSize.Y - 40) / FULL, 0.55, 1)
        scale.Scale = s
        return s
    end
    local s0 = Fit() or 1
    main.Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0.5, -math.floor(FULL * s0 / 2))

    if Workspace.CurrentCamera then
        table.insert(GlobalConnections,
            Workspace.CurrentCamera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(Fit))
    end

    badge.Activated:Connect(function()
        minimized = not minimized
        TweenService:Create(main,
            TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(WIDTH, minimized and MINI or FULL) }):Play()
        badge.Text = minimized and "[+]" or "[DK]"
    end)

    local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil

    header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging  = true
            dragStart = input.Position
            startPos  = main.Position
            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                    dragging = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    table.insert(GlobalConnections,
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if not dragging or not dragStart or not startPos then return end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                main.Position = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
            end
        end))

    table.insert(GlobalConnections,
        UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
            if processed then return end
            if input.KeyCode == CFG.UIToggleKey then
                main.Visible = not main.Visible
            end
        end))

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- toasts
    ------------------------------------------------------------

    local toastHolder = Make("Frame", {
        AnchorPoint            = Vector2.new(1, 1),
        Position               = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16),
        Size                   = UDim2.fromOffset(310, 320),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Parent                 = gui,
    }, {
        Make("UIListLayout", {
            VerticalAlignment   = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom,
            HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right,
            Padding             = UDim.new(0, 8),
            SortOrder           = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        }),
    })

    local toastCount = 0
    local liveToasts = {}

    function UI.Toast(category, message)

        if not CFG.Notifications or not toastHolder.Parent then return end

        toastCount += 1
        local color = ColorFor(category)

        local slot = Make("Frame", {
            Size                   = UDim2.fromOffset(310, 58),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            LayoutOrder            = toastCount,
            Parent                 = toastHolder,
        })
        local body = Make("Frame", {
            Size                   = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1),
            Position               = UDim2.fromOffset(340, 0),
            BackgroundColor3       = Theme.Bg,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.03,
            Parent                 = slot,
        }, { Corner(8) })
        Stroke(color, 1.2, 0.2).Parent = body

        Make("Frame", {
            Size             = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -14),
            Position         = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 7),
            BackgroundColor3 = color,
            BorderSizePixel  = 0,
            Parent           = body,
        }, { Corner(2) })

        Label({
            Text       = "ANTIKILL  -  BY DINOKID",
            TextSize   = 11,
            TextColor3 = color,
            Position   = UDim2.fromOffset(19, 7),
            Size       = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 16),
            Parent     = body,
        })
        Label({
            Text           = category .. ": " .. tostring(message),
            TextSize       = 12,
            TextWrapped    = true,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
            Position       = UDim2.fromOffset(19, 25),
            Size           = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 28),
            Parent         = body,
        })

        TweenService:Create(body,
            TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }):Play()

        table.insert(liveToasts, slot)
        while #liveToasts > 4 do
            local oldest = table.remove(liveToasts, 1)
            if oldest then oldest:Destroy() end
        end

        task.delay(3.2, function()
            if not slot.Parent then return end
            local out = TweenService:Create(body,
                TweenInfo.new(0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
                { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(340, 0) })
            out:Play()
            out.Completed:Wait()
            local i = table.find(liveToasts, slot)
            if i then table.remove(liveToasts, i) end
            slot:Destroy()
        end)
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- live log feed
    ------------------------------------------------------------

    local feedItems = {}
    local feedCount = 0
    local dimHex = Theme.Dim:ToHex()

    function UI.Feed(entry)

        if not feed.Parent then return end

        feedCount += 1

        local line = string.format(
            '<font color="#%s">%6.1f</font>  <font color="#%s">%s</font>  %s',
            dimHex, entry.t, ColorFor(entry.cat):ToHex(),
            Escape(entry.cat), Escape(entry.msg))

        local item = Label({
            Text           = line,
            RichText       = true,
            TextSize       = 11,
            TextWrapped    = true,
            TextTruncate   = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
            AutomaticSize  = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            Size           = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
            LayoutOrder    = -feedCount,
            Parent         = feed,
        })

        table.insert(feedItems, item)
        while #feedItems > 45 do
            local oldest = table.remove(feedItems, 1)
            if oldest then oldest:Destroy() end
        end

        feedCountText.Text = feedCount .. " events"
    end

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- live refresh
    ------------------------------------------------------------

    local function CountOwnTools()
        local n = 0
        for _, container in ipairs({ Backpack or false, Character or false }) do
            if container then
                for _, t in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if t:IsA("Tool") and not IsForeignTool(t) then n += 1 end
                end
            end
        end
        return n
    end

    task.spawn(function()

        local dots = 0

        while Alive and gui.Parent do

            dots = dots % 3 + 1
            local now = os.clock()
            local text, color

            if ReanimMode then
                text, color = "REANIMATED // DRIVER GUARDED", Theme.Cyan
            elseif OwnershipLost then
                text, color = "BODY TAKEN OVER // CUTTING LINKS", Theme.Red
            elseif WasAnchored then
                text, color = "FROZEN // TOOL LINK CUT", Theme.Red
            elseif now < LockdownUntil then
                text, color = string.format("LOCKDOWN // %.1fs", LockdownUntil - now), Theme.Yellow
            elseif TouchOff then
                text, color = "DROPPED " .. string.upper(NearDropped and NearDropped.Name or "TOOL")
                    .. " NEAR // TOUCH OFF", Theme.Yellow
            elseif not Root or not Root.Parent then
                text, color = "WAITING FOR CHARACTER" .. string.rep(".", dots), Theme.Dim
            else
                text, color = "SHIELD ARMED // SCANNING" .. string.rep(".", dots), Theme.Green
            end

            statusText.Text       = text
            statusText.TextColor3 = color
            sweep.BackgroundColor3 = color
            iconStroke.Color      = color
            icon.Text = ReanimMode and "🦖" or (color == Theme.Red and "⚠️" or "🔒")

            local upright = Root ~= nil and Root.Parent ~= nil
                and Root.CFrame.UpVector.Y > 0.5
            bodyLine.Text = string.format("> BODY // %s  |  %s",
                OwnershipState, upright and "UPRIGHT" or "DOWN")
            bodyLine.TextColor3 = (OwnershipState == "LOST" or not upright)
                and Theme.Red or Theme.Green

            toolLine.Text = string.format("> TOOLS // %d yours  |  %d hidden  |  %d blocked",
                CountOwnTools(), #HiddenTools, Stats.ToolBlocked)

            tiles.BLOCKS.Text   = tostring(Stats.TotalBlocks)
            tiles.TOOLKILL.Text = tostring(Stats.ToolBlocked)
            tiles.LINKS.Text    = tostring(Stats.GripSevered + Stats.Assembly
                + Stats.Tether + Stats.Joint)
            tiles.SAVES.Text    = tostring(Stats.Recoveries + Stats.Teleport
                + Stats.PositionAssert + Stats.StateSaves + Stats.OwnershipLost)

            hiddenText.Text = "HIDDEN TOOLS: " .. #HiddenTools

            if reanimButton and host then
                local okState, st = pcall(host.State)
                st = okState and st or "?"
                local label, tint
                if st == "reanimated" then
                    label, tint = "DEANIMATE", Theme.Red
                elseif st == "starting" then
                    label, tint = "STARTING...", Theme.Yellow
                elseif st == "deanimating" then
                    label, tint = "RESPAWNING...", Theme.Yellow
                else
                    label, tint = "REANIMATE", Theme.Green
                end
                reanimButton.Text = label
                reanimButton.TextColor3 = tint
                reanimStroke.Color = tint
            end

            local worst = nil
            for _, data in pairs(PlayerThreats) do
                if data.Flagged and data.Player and data.Player.Parent
                    and (not worst or data.Score > worst.Score) then
                    worst = data
                end
            end
            if worst then
                footLeft.Text = "THREAT: " .. worst.Player.Name
                footLeft.TextColor3 = Theme.Red
            else
                footLeft.Text = "Gui Ready"
                footLeft.TextColor3 = Theme.Green
            end

            task.wait(0.2)
        end
    end)

    ------------------------------------------------------------
    -- go live
    ------------------------------------------------------------

    UI.Built = true
    UIHooks.Feed  = UI.Feed
    UIHooks.Toast = UI.Toast

    -- what happened before the panel existed
    for i = math.max(1, #EventLog - 20), #EventLog do
        if EventLog[i] then UI.Feed(EventLog[i]) end
    end

    UI.Toast("SECURE", "shield armed - " .. CFG.UIToggleKey.Name .. " shows / hides the panel")
end


--============================================================
-- STOP   (a re-run calls this on the old copy)
--============================================================

_G.DinoAntikillStop = function()

    Alive = false

    for _, connection in ipairs(GlobalConnections) do
        pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
    end
    table.clear(GlobalConnections)

    DisconnectCharacterConnections()
    ClearAllShields()

    if TouchOff then SetBodyTouch(true) end
    ForceReleaseAnchor()

    CFG.StateLock = false
    ApplyStateLock()

    UIHooks.Feed, UIHooks.Toast = nil, nil
    if UI.Gui then pcall(function() UI.Gui:Destroy() end) end
end

-- setup() calls this after the split: protect the driver you walk
-- with (same character, new Humanoid / HumanoidRootPart).
AntiFling.Rebind = function()
    local c = LocalPlayer.Character
    if c then SetupCharacter(c, true) end
end

AntiFling.Grace = function(seconds)
    TeleportGraceUntil = os.clock() + math.max(0, tonumber(seconds) or 1)
end

-- the reanimate's messages as toasts on this panel
AntiFling.Toast = function(category, message)
    if UIHooks.Toast then pcall(UIHooks.Toast, tostring(category), tostring(message)) end
end

    end)

task.spawn(function()
    local ok, err = pcall(startAntikill)
    if not ok then warn("[ANTIKILL] failed to start: " .. tostring(err)) end
end)


--==================================================
-- BOOT
--==================================================

-- First thing the script does: -ch, then a pause before anything else.
Ext.bootBody = lp.Character
if START_COMMAND and Ext.sendOnce(Ext.bootBody, START_COMMAND) then
    warn("[Reanim] sent " .. START_COMMAND)
    task.wait(START_WAIT)
end

-- this body is handled here, so the CharacterAdded that the reassign
-- below fires for it is not mistaken for a respawn
if Ext.bootBody then Ext.handled[Ext.bootBody] = true end

-- a body the replaced copy already reanimated is refreshed instead
if not (Ext.bootBody and Ext.bootBody:FindFirstChild("Corpse")) then
    respawnrequest()
end
task.spawn(buildUI)

-- -gh → wait → poll for the rig → split. Shared by first load and respawn.
local bootToken = 0

local function bootCharacter(c, sendStart)
    if not Ext.alive() then return end
    bootToken += 1
    local my = bootToken

    if sendStart and START_COMMAND and Ext.sendOnce(c, START_COMMAND) then
        warn("[Reanim] sent " .. START_COMMAND)
        task.wait(START_WAIT)
        if my ~= bootToken or not c.Parent or not Ext.alive() then return end
    end

    -- don't split a half-loaded character
    c:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
    local bootHum = c:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
    if my ~= bootToken or not c.Parent or not Ext.alive() then return end

    local bootR15 = (bootHum and bootHum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15)
        or c:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") ~= nil
    warn("[Reanim] detected body type: " .. (bootR15 and "R15" or "R6"))

    local bootStart = os.clock()

    if RIG.enabled then
        warn("[Reanim] sending -gh for the rig")
        Ext.sendOnce(c, "-gh " .. GH_IDS)

        -- GH_WAIT also covers the respawnrequest settle time
        local waitFor = math.max(GH_WAIT, SETUP_DELAY)
        warn("[Reanim] waiting " .. waitFor .. " s for the rig parts …")
        task.wait(waitFor)
        if my ~= bootToken or not c.Parent or not Ext.alive() then return end

        -- keep looking if something is still missing
        local found, missing = countRigParts(c)
        local deadline = os.clock() + RIG_EXTRA_WAIT
        while found < #RIG.parts and os.clock() < deadline do
            task.wait(0.5)
            if my ~= bootToken or not c.Parent or not Ext.alive() then return end
            found, missing = countRigParts(c)
        end

        if found < #RIG.parts then
            warn(string.format("[Reanim] %d/%d rig parts present, continuing anyway · missing: %s",
                found, #RIG.parts, table.concat(missing, ", ")))
        else
            warn("[Reanim] all rig parts present")
        end
    else
        -- Real body: wait until your avatar (and every accessory on it)
        -- has finished loading, so all of it gets captured and driven.
        if not lp:HasAppearanceLoaded() then
            warn("[Reanim] waiting for your avatar to finish loading …")
            local loaded = false
            local conn = lp.CharacterAppearanceLoaded:Connect(function() loaded = true end)
            local deadline = os.clock() + 15
            while not loaded and not lp:HasAppearanceLoaded() and os.clock() < deadline do
                task.wait(0.2)
            end
            conn:Disconnect()
        end
        if my ~= bootToken or not c.Parent or not Ext.alive() then return end
        warn("[Reanim] avatar loaded")
    end

    -- make sure everything has settled on you before the commands
    task.wait(CONFIRM_WAIT)
    if my ~= bootToken or not c.Parent or not Ext.alive() then return end

    for _, cmd in ipairs(BOOT_COMMANDS) do
        if Ext.sendOnce(c, cmd) then
            warn("[Reanim] sent " .. cmd)
            task.wait(BOOT_COMMAND_GAP)
        else
            warn("[Reanim] " .. cmd .. " was already sent for this body, skipped")
        end
        if my ~= bootToken or not c.Parent or not Ext.alive() then return end
    end

    task.wait(POST_CMD_WAIT)
    if my ~= bootToken or not c.Parent or not Ext.alive() then return end

    -- never split before respawnrequest has settled (see SETUP_DELAY)
    local settle = SETUP_DELAY - (os.clock() - bootStart)
    if settle > 0 then task.wait(settle) end
    if my ~= bootToken or not c.Parent or not Ext.alive() then return end

    setup(c)
    warn(RIG.enabled and "[Reanim] ready — Right Shift → RIG to tune the parts"
        or "[Reanim] ready — real body reanimated · Right Shift opens the panel")
end


--==================================================
-- DEANIMATE / REANIMATE
--==================================================
-- The real body is dead with broken joints, so "normal again" means a
-- fresh body: DEANIMATE asks the server for the respawn (the same way
-- the reset button does while reanimated), skips the reanimate on the
-- new body and puts it back where you were. REANIMATE runs the whole
-- boot again on whatever body you have.
--==================================================

-- Gets you a new body: the game's respawn commands first (they also end
-- its -pd permadeath), then the engine's character reassign. onFail
-- runs if nothing arrived after every try.
function Ext.requestRespawn(from, onFail)
    from = from or lp.Character
    task.spawn(function()
        local function arrived()
            local now = lp.Character
            return now ~= nil and now ~= from
        end

        local function waitForBody()
            local t0 = os.clock()
            while os.clock() - t0 < Ext.respawnWait do
                if arrived() then return true end
                task.wait(0.25)
            end
            return arrived()
        end

        for _, cmd in ipairs(Ext.respawnCommands or {}) do
            warn("[Reanim] respawning · sending " .. cmd)
            send(cmd)
            if waitForBody() then return end
        end

        -- last resort: the reassign trick (the body has to be dead)
        warn("[Reanim] the game didn't respawn you · trying the reassign")
        if from and from.Parent and not from:FindFirstChild("Corpse") then
            local h = from:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then pcall(function() h.Health = 0 end) end
            task.wait(0.15)
        end
        respawnrequest()
        task.wait(Players.RespawnTime)
        if waitForBody() then return end

        if onFail then onFail() end
    end)
end

function Ext.deanimate()
    local st = Ext.state()
    if st == "normal" then return false, "already a normal player" end
    if st == "deanimating" then return false, "already respawning" end

    local c = lp.Character
    Ext.enabled  = false
    Ext.busy     = "deanimating"
    Ext.oldChar  = c
    bootToken   += 1                 -- cancels a boot still in progress
    local root   = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    Ext.returnCF = root and root.CFrame or nil
    if Ext.rbxStop then pcall(Ext.rbxStop) end
    Ext.changed()
    warn("[Reanim] deanimating · respawning you as a normal player")

    pcall(function() sg:SetCore("ResetButtonCallback", true) end)

    Ext.requestRespawn(c, function()
        -- nothing brought a new body: stay reanimated rather than stuck
        if Ext.busy ~= "deanimating" then return end
        Ext.busy, Ext.oldChar, Ext.returnCF = nil, nil, nil
        Ext.enabled = true
        warn("[Reanim] couldn't respawn you: the game ignored "
            .. table.concat(Ext.respawnCommands, " / ")
            .. " · you're still reanimated · type -re yourself, or set Ext.respawnCommands to this game's respawn command")
        Ext.changed()
    end)
    return true
end

function Ext.reanimate()
    if Ext.busy then return false, "still respawning, try again in a moment" end
    if Ext.enabled then return false, "already reanimated" end

    local c = lp.Character
    local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not (c and c.Parent and h and h.Health > 0) then
        return false, "wait until you have a living body"
    end

    Ext.enabled = true
    Ext.oldChar = nil
    Ext.changed()
    warn("[Reanim] reanimating …")

    respawnrequest()
    task.spawn(bootCharacter, c, START_COMMAND_ON_RESPAWN)
    return true
end

function Ext.toggle()
    if Ext.state() == "normal" then return Ext.reanimate() end
    return Ext.deanimate()
end

-- Feedback in the log and, when the ANTIKILL panel is up, as a toast
function Ext.notify(text)
    warn("[Reanim] " .. tostring(text))
    if AntiFling.Toast then pcall(AntiFling.Toast, "REANIM", tostring(text)) end
end

--==================================================
-- -play <id or name> · -stop
--==================================================

-- "-play 507771019", "-play 507.771.019" (dots get past the chat filter
-- that hides long numbers from other players), "-play dance2", or the
-- name of any animation in your list.
function Ext.play(text)
    text = tostring(text or "")
    local plain = string.lower((string.gsub(text, "%s", "")))

    -- an animation you already have, by name
    for name in pairs(Anims) do
        if string.lower(name) == plain then
            editPose, editorPaused = nil, false
            manualAnim = name
            playAnim(name, true)
            return true, "playing " .. name
        end
    end

    local id = (string.gsub(text, "%D", ""))
    if #id < 5 then
        local key = (plain == "dance1") and "dance" or plain
        if Ext.RBX_EMOTE and Ext.RBX_EMOTE[key] then
            id = Ext.RBX_IDS[IS_R15 and "R15" or "R6"][key]
        else
            if string.find(text, "#", 1, true) then
                return false, "Roblox's chat filter hid that id; write it with dots, like -play 507.771.019"
            end
            return false, 'no animation id or name in "' .. text .. '"'
        end
    end

    -- deanimated: play it on your normal body (Roblox replicates that)
    if Ext.state() == "normal" then
        local c = lp.Character
        local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        if not animator then return false, "your body has no Animator" end
        if Ext.realTrack then pcall(Ext.realTrack.Stop, Ext.realTrack, 0.2) end
        local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
        anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. id
        local ok, track = pcall(function() return animator:LoadAnimation(anim) end)
        if not (ok and track) then return false, "couldn't load animation " .. id end
        pcall(function() track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action end)
        pcall(track.Play, track, 0.2)
        Ext.realTrack = track
        return true, "playing " .. id .. " on your normal body"
    end

    -- reanimated: already imported once, play it straight away
    local name = "anim_" .. id
    if Anims[name] then
        editPose, editorPaused = nil, false
        manualAnim = name
        playAnim(name, true)
        return true, "playing " .. name
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, res, info = AnimImport.loadAndPlay(id, name)
        if ok then
            Ext.notify("playing " .. tostring(res) .. (info and (" · " .. info) or ""))
        else
            Ext.notify("couldn't play " .. id .. ": " .. tostring(res))
        end
    end)
    return true, "loading animation " .. id .. " …"
end

function Ext.stopPlay()
    manualAnim = nil
    if Ext.realTrack then
        pcall(Ext.realTrack.Stop, Ext.realTrack, 0.2)
        Ext.realTrack = nil
    end
    local R = Ext.rbx
    if R and R.emote then
        pcall(R.emote.Stop, R.emote, 0.2)
        R.emote = nil
    end
end

--==================================================
-- CHAT COMMANDS
--==================================================
-- Yours:   -remove <part> · -unremove <part> · -play <id|name> · -stop ·
--          -deanimate · -reanimate · -anims roblox|custom · /e <emote>
-- Others:  -remove · -unremove · -play · -stop act on YOUR body while
--          Ext.publicCommands is on (BODY tab). One command per player
--          every Ext.publicCooldown seconds; Ext.blocked shuts anyone out.
--==================================================

Ext.publicCommands = true
Ext.publicCooldown = 1.5
Ext.blocked        = {}      -- [lowercase name] = true or [UserId] = true
Ext.lastBy         = {}

local PUBLIC_VERBS = { remove = true, unremove = true, restore = true, play = true, stop = true }

function Ext.command(msg, sender)
    local owner = (sender == nil or sender == lp)

    local lower = string.lower(tostring(msg or ""))
    lower = string.gsub(lower, "^%s+", "")
    lower = string.gsub(lower, "%s+$", "")

    local verb, rest = string.match(lower, "^%-(%a+)%s*(.*)$")

    if not owner then
        if not (Ext.publicCommands and verb and PUBLIC_VERBS[verb]) then return end
        if Ext.blocked[string.lower(sender.Name)] or Ext.blocked[sender.UserId] then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        local last = Ext.lastBy[sender.UserId]
        if last and now - last < Ext.publicCooldown then return end
        Ext.lastBy[sender.UserId] = now
    end

    local by = owner and "" or (sender.DisplayName .. ": ")

    if verb == "remove" and rest ~= "" then
        local parts, err = Ext.setHidden(rest, true)
        Ext.notify(by .. (parts and ("hid " .. table.concat(parts, ", ")) or err))
        return
    end

    if (verb == "unremove" or verb == "restore") and rest ~= "" then
        local parts, err = Ext.setHidden(rest, false)
        Ext.notify(by .. (parts and ("showed " .. table.concat(parts, ", ")) or err))
        return
    end

    if verb == "play" and rest ~= "" then
        local ok, info = Ext.play(rest)
        Ext.notify(by .. tostring(info or (ok and "playing" or "failed")))
        return
    end

    if verb == "stop" and rest == "" then
        Ext.stopPlay()
        Ext.notify(by .. "stopped the animation")
        return
    end

    if not owner then return end

    if lower == "-deanimate" then
        local ok, err = Ext.deanimate()
        if not ok and err then warn("[Reanim] " .. err) end
        return
    end
    if lower == "-reanimate" then
        local ok, err = Ext.reanimate()
        if not ok and err then warn("[Reanim] " .. err) end
        return
    end

    local arg = string.match(lower, "^%-anims?%s+(%a+)$")
    if arg then
        if arg == "roblox" or arg == "default" or arg == "rbx" then
            Ext.setAnimMode("roblox")
        elseif arg == "custom" or arg == "own" or arg == "mine" then
            Ext.setAnimMode("custom")
        end
        return
    end

    arg = string.match(lower, "^/e%s+(%S+)") or string.match(lower, "^/emote%s+(%S+)")
    if arg then Ext.rbxEmote(arg) end
end

do
    -- your own messages
    local lastMsg, lastAt = nil, 0
    local function onOwnMessage(msg)
        if not Ext.alive() then return end
        msg = tostring(msg or "")
        local now = os.clock()
        if msg == lastMsg and now - lastAt < 0.6 then return end
        lastMsg, lastAt = msg, now
        local ok, err = pcall(Ext.command, msg, nil)
        if not ok then warn("[Reanim] chat command failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
    end
    lp.Chatted:Connect(onOwnMessage)
    pcall(function()
        TextChatService.SendingMessage:Connect(function(m) onOwnMessage(m.Text) end)
    end)

    -- everyone else's (TextChatService and the legacy chat, deduplicated)
    local seen, seenCount = {}, 0
    local function onOtherMessage(player, text)
        if not Ext.alive() then return end
        if not player or player == lp then return end
        text = tostring(text or "")
        local trimmed = string.gsub(text, "^%s+", "")
        if string.sub(trimmed, 1, 1) ~= "-" then return end
        local key = player.UserId .. "|" .. text
        local now = os.clock()
        if seen[key] and now - seen[key] < 1 then return end
        seen[key] = now
        seenCount += 1
        if seenCount > 200 then
            table.clear(seen)
            seenCount = 0
        end
        local ok, err = pcall(Ext.command, text, player)
        if not ok then warn("[Reanim] chat command failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
    end

    pcall(function()
        TextChatService.MessageReceived:Connect(function(m)
            local src = m.TextSource
            if not src then return end
            onOtherMessage(Players:GetPlayerByUserId(src.UserId), m.Text)
        end)
    end)

    local function hook(p)
        if p == lp then return end
        p.Chatted:Connect(function(text) onOtherMessage(p, text) end)
    end
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do hook(p) end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(hook)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local c = lp.Character or lp.CharacterAdded:Wait()
    if Ext.bootBody ~= c then
        -- arrived after load: the respawn handler boots it
        return
    end
    if c:FindFirstChild("Corpse") then
        -- The copy this run replaced had already reanimated this body.
        -- A split body can't be handed over, so get a fresh one; the
        -- respawn handler reanimates it.
        warn("[Reanim] this body was reanimated by the copy you replaced · refreshing you, then reanimating")
        Ext.requestRespawn(c, function()
            warn("[Reanim] couldn't refresh you · respawn (-re) and the reanimate starts by itself")
        end)
        return
    end
    bootCharacter(c)
end)

lp.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
    task.wait()
    if not Ext.alive() then return end

    -- our own reassign of the body being left behind
    if Ext.oldChar and newChar == Ext.oldChar then return end

    -- respawnrequest() hands the server the SAME body again, and that
    -- fires CharacterAdded too. Treating it as a respawn called
    -- respawnrequest() again, which fired it again: an endless loop
    -- (stuck camera, lag, the boot restarting forever after the start
    -- line). Only a body we haven't seen yet is a real respawn.
    if Ext.handled[newChar] then return end
    Ext.handled[newChar] = true

    if Ext.enabled then respawnrequest() end

    corpseParts   = {}
    hatData       = {}
    Props         = {}
    camHeadPos    = nil
    corpseRoot    = nil
    rigHandles    = {}
    rigSpares     = {}
    lastLimbPose  = {}
    rebuildPoseParts()

    if not Ext.enabled then
        -- deanimated: a normal body, nothing to boot. Put it back where
        -- you were standing.
        Ext.busy, Ext.oldChar = nil, nil
        local cf = Ext.returnCF
        Ext.returnCF = nil
        if cf then
            task.spawn(function()
                local r = newChar:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
                if not (r and newChar.Parent) then return end
                pcall(function()
                    if _G.AntiFlingTeleportGrace then _G.AntiFlingTeleportGrace(3) end
                end)
                task.wait(0.1)
                pcall(function() newChar:PivotTo(cf + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)) end)
            end)
        end
        warn("[Reanim] deanimated · normal player · REANIMATE (BODY tab, header, ANTIKILL panel or -reanimate) to go back")
        Ext.changed()
        return
    end

    bootCharacter(newChar, START_COMMAND_ON_RESPAWN)
end)
